\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{5}{2}-\alpha-\frac{1}{2}\beta}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{11}{8}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha}\right).

(3.34)

\displaystyle I_{12}=~

\displaystyle CK_{1}\bar{\rho}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq C(K)\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha},

(3.35)

\displaystyle I_{13}=~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\left(K_{1}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+K_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}K_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\left(C(K)\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}+K_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}K_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(K)\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\left(\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}(\alpha+\beta)-1}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-1}\right),

(3.36)

Then, substituting (
3.1.1
)–(
3.1.1
) into (
3.1.1
), we have

\displaystyle\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,\sigma(T)]}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}K_{1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}+C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{5}{2}-\alpha-\frac{1}{2}\beta}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{11}{8}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}(\alpha+\beta)-1}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle K_{1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha},

(3.37)

provided that
\alpha>\beta-2
,

C(K)\bar{\rho}\leq\min\left\{\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1},\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{5}(4\alpha+\beta-5)},\bar{\theta}^{2(\alpha-\beta+2)}\right\},

and

C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{5}{2}-\alpha-\frac{1}{2}\beta}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{11}{8}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}(\alpha+\beta)-1}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-1}\right)\leq\frac{1}{2}K_{1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha},

this complete the proof of (
3.18
).

Next, we will prove (
3.19
). Multiplying
(\ref{FCNS:2})_{3}
by
\theta_{t}
and integrating the resulting equality over
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
, we obtain after integration by parts that

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa\beta}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int\theta^{\beta}|\nabla\theta|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\int\rho|\dot{\theta}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\int\rho\dot{\theta}u\cdot\nabla\theta{\mathrm{d}}x+\lambda\int\theta^{\alpha}({\rm div}u)^{2}\dot{\theta}{\mathrm{d}}x+2\mu\int\theta^{\alpha}|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}\dot{\theta}{\mathrm{d}}x-R\int\rho\theta{\rm div}u\dot{\theta}{\mathrm{d}}x+R\int\rho\theta u\cdot\nabla\theta{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-\lambda\int\theta^{\alpha}u\cdot\nabla\theta({\rm div}u)^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x-2\mu\int\theta^{\alpha}u\cdot\nabla\theta|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{1}{2}\kappa\beta\int\theta^{\beta-1}\theta_{t}|\nabla\theta|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle:=~

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{i=1}^{8}J_{i},

(3.38)

where the terms
J_{1}
to
J_{8}
can be estimated as follows.

\displaystyle J_{1}+J_{4}\leq~

\displaystyle\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\int|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}|\left(|u\cdot\nabla\theta|+\bar{\theta}|\nabla u|\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}\left(\|u\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}+\bar{\theta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

(3.39)

\displaystyle J_{2}+J_{3}\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int|\dot{\theta}||\nabla u|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\leq C\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{3}{2}},

(3.40)

\displaystyle J_{5}\leq C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}},

(3.41)

\displaystyle J_{6}+J_{7}\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int|u\cdot\nabla\theta||\nabla u|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2},

(3.42)

and

\displaystyle J_{8}\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\int|\dot{\theta}||\nabla\theta|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\int|u||\nabla\theta|^{3}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{2\beta-2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{3}+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{2\beta-1}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{2\beta-1}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(K)\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}.

(3.43)

At last, we substitute (
3.1.1
)–(
3.1.1
) into (
3.1.1
) and integrate the resulting inequality over
(0,\sigma(T))
. Noting that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{-2\beta}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\left(\bar{\rho}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{4}}^{4}+\bar{\theta}^{2\beta-2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}^{4}\right){\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{-2\beta}\left(K_{2}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\beta}+\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{3-\alpha}+\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{3}{\mathrm{d}}t+\bar{\theta}^{2\beta-2}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{3}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle CK_{2}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\beta}+C\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{3-\alpha-2\beta}+C(K)^{\frac{1}{2}}K_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha-2\beta}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+CK_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-1}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\beta}+\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{3-\alpha-2\beta}+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-2\beta}+C(K)\bar{\theta}^{-1-\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(K)\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\beta},

(3.44)

we obtain

\displaystyle\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,\sigma(T)]}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla\theta_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}+\bar{\theta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{2\beta-1}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C(K)\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla\theta_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(K)\big(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{2-\beta}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}(\alpha+\beta)}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{7}{4}-\alpha}+\bar{\rho}+\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{4}\beta-\frac{1}{4}\alpha-1}\big)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle K_{2}\bar{\theta}^{\beta},

(3.45)

provided that
\beta>\alpha
and

C(K)\leq\min\left\{\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}(\alpha+\beta-3)},\bar{\theta}^{2(\beta-\alpha)}\right\},

where we have used (
3.6
) and (
3.30
). Hence, we complete the proof of (
3.19
).
∎

Higher-order estimates of velocity and temperature on time interval
[0,\sigma(T)]
are given in the following lemma.

Lemma 3.4
.

Under the conditions of Proposition
3
, there exists positive constants
K_{3}
,
K_{4}
and
L_{2}
depending on
\mu
,
\lambda
,
\kappa
,
R
and
C_{0}
, such that if
(\rho,u,\theta)
is a smooth solution of (
1.15
)–(
1.16
) on
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}\times(0,T]
, the following estimate holds

A_{3}(\sigma(T))=\sup\limits_{t\in[0,\sigma(T)]}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq K_{3}\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha},

(3.46)

A_{4}(\sigma(T))=\sup\limits_{t\in[0,\sigma(T)]}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq K_{4}\bar{\theta}^{2\beta},

(3.47)

provided that
\bar{\theta}\geq L_{2}
,
2\leq\alpha<\beta<\alpha+2
and (
3.20
) holds.

Proof.

Operating
\dot{u}^{j}(\partial_{t}+\mathrm{div}(u\cdot))
to
(\ref{FCNS:2})_{2}^{j}
, summing with respect to
j
, and integrating the resulting equation over
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int\rho|\dot{u}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x=~

\displaystyle-\int\dot{u}^{j}\left(\partial_{j}P_{t}+{\rm div}(u\partial_{j}P)\right){\mathrm{d}}x+2\mu\int\dot{u}^{j}\left[\partial_{t}({\rm div}(\theta^{\alpha}\mathfrak{D}(u)))+{\rm div}(u{\rm div}(\theta^{\alpha}\mathfrak{D}(u)))\right]{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\lambda\int\dot{u}^{j}\left[\partial_{t}(\partial_{j}(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u))+{\rm div}(u\cdot\partial_{j}(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u))\right]{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle:=~

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}M_{i}.

(3.48)

First, from
[
62
]
and (
3.22
), using integration by parts, we can see that

\displaystyle M_{1}=~

\displaystyle-\int\dot{u}^{j}\left(\partial_{j}P_{t}+{\rm div}(u\partial_{j}P)\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle R\int\rho\dot{\theta}{\rm div}\dot{u}{\mathrm{d}}x+\int P\left(\partial_{k}\left(u^{k}{\rm div}\dot{u}\right)-{\rm div}u{\rm div}\dot{u}-\partial_{j}\left(\partial_{k}\dot{u}^{j}u^{k}\right)\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle R\int\rho\dot{\theta}{\rm div}\dot{u}{\mathrm{d}}x-R\int\rho\theta\partial_{k}\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{j}u^{k}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{\mu}{8}\left(\frac{\bar{\theta}}{2}\right)^{\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\left(\|\rho\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\rho\theta\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{\mu}{8}\left(\frac{\bar{\theta}}{2}\right)^{\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha+2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(3.49)

Next, we have

\displaystyle M_{2}=~

\displaystyle\mu\int\dot{u}^{j}\left(\partial_{t}\partial_{i}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right)+\partial_{k}\left(u^{k}\partial_{i}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right)\right)\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\mu\int\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{t}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right){\mathrm{d}}x+\mu\int\dot{u}^{j}{\rm div}u\partial_{i}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\mu\int\dot{u}^{j}u^{k}\partial_{i}\partial_{k}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\mu\int\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{t}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right){\mathrm{d}}x-\mu\int\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}{\rm div}u\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-\mu\int\theta^{\alpha}\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{i}({\rm div}u)(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i}){\mathrm{d}}x-\mu\int\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}u^{k}\partial_{k}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-\mu\int\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{i}u^{k}\partial_{k}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\mu\int\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{t}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right){\mathrm{d}}x-\mu\int\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}{\rm div}u\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-\mu\int\theta^{\alpha}\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{i}({\rm div}u)(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i}){\mathrm{d}}x-\mu\int\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}u^{k}\partial_{k}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\mu\int\partial_{k}\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{i}u^{k}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right){\mathrm{d}}x+\mu\int\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{k}\partial_{i}u^{k}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\mu\int\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{t}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right){\mathrm{d}}x-\mu\int\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}u^{k}\partial_{k}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\mu\int\theta^{\alpha}\partial_{k}\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{i}u^{k}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i}){\mathrm{d}}x-\mu\int\theta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}{\rm div}u(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i}){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle-2\mu\int\theta^{\alpha}|\mathfrak{D}(\dot{u})|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int|\nabla\dot{u}||\nabla u|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+C\int\theta^{\alpha-1}|\dot{\theta}||\nabla\dot{u}||\nabla u|{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{4}\mu\left(\frac{\bar{\theta}}{2}\right)^{\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{4}}^{4}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-2}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla{\dot{\theta}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(3.50)

where we have used the fact

\displaystyle-\mu\int\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}\left(\partial_{t}\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right)+u\cdot\nabla\left(\theta^{\alpha}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right)\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\mu\int\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}\left((\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})(\partial_{t}(\theta^{\alpha})+u\cdot\nabla(\theta^{\alpha}))+\theta^{\alpha}\left((\partial_{i}u^{j}_{t}+\partial_{j}u^{i}_{t})+u\cdot\nabla(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right)\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\mu\int\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}\left(\alpha\theta^{\alpha-1}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})(\theta_{t}+u\cdot\nabla\theta)+\theta^{\alpha}\left((\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}+\partial_{j}\dot{u}^{i})+u\cdot\nabla(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i})\right)\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-\mu\int\theta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}\left(\partial_{i}(u\cdot\nabla)u^{j}+\partial_{j}(u\cdot\nabla)u^{i}\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\mu\alpha\int\theta^{\alpha-1}\dot{\theta}\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i}){\mathrm{d}}x-\mu\int\theta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}\left(\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}+\partial_{j}\dot{u}^{i}\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\mu\int\theta^{\alpha}\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}\left(\partial_{i}u^{k}\partial_{k}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{k}\partial_{k}u^{i}\right){\mathrm{d}}x.

Similarly, we get

\displaystyle M_{3}=~

\displaystyle\lambda\int\dot{u}^{j}\left(\partial_{t}\partial_{j}(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u)+{\rm div}(u\partial_{j}(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u))\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\lambda\int\partial_{j}\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{t}(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u){\mathrm{d}}x+\lambda\int\dot{u}^{j}\left({\rm div}u\partial_{j}(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u)+u\cdot\nabla\partial_{j}(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u)\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\lambda\int{\rm div}\dot{u}\partial_{t}(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u){\mathrm{d}}x-\lambda\int\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}\dot{u}({\rm div}u)^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x-\lambda\int\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{j}({\rm div}u){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-\lambda\int{\rm div}\dot{u}u\cdot\nabla(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u){\mathrm{d}}x-\lambda\int\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{j}u^{k}\partial_{k}(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\lambda\int{\rm div}\dot{u}\partial_{t}(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u){\mathrm{d}}x-\lambda\int{\rm div}\dot{u}u\cdot\nabla(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u){\mathrm{d}}x-\lambda\int\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}\dot{u}({\rm div}u)^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-\lambda\int\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{j}({\rm div}u){\mathrm{d}}x+\lambda\int\theta^{\alpha}\partial_{k}\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{j}u^{k}{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x+\lambda\int\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{j}({\rm div}u){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\lambda\alpha\int\theta^{\alpha-1}{\rm div}\dot{u}{\rm div}u\dot{\theta}{\mathrm{d}}x-\lambda\int\theta^{\alpha}({\rm div}\dot{u})^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\lambda\int\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}\dot{u}\partial_{i}u^{j}\partial_{j}u^{i}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-\lambda\int\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}\dot{u}({\rm div}u)^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\lambda\int\theta^{\alpha}\partial_{k}\dot{u}^{j}\partial_{j}u^{k}{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle-\lambda\left(\frac{\bar{\theta}}{2}\right)^{\alpha}\|{\rm div}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\mu}{4}\left(\frac{\bar{\theta}}{2}\right)^{\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{4}}^{4}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-2}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla{\dot{\theta}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(3.51)

where we have used the fact

\displaystyle-\lambda\int{\rm div}\dot{u}\left(\partial_{t}(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u)+u\cdot\nabla(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u)\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\lambda\int{\rm div}\dot{u}\left({\rm div}u(\partial_{t}(\theta^{\alpha})+u\cdot\nabla(\theta^{\alpha}))+\theta^{\alpha}({\rm div}u_{t}+u\cdot\nabla({\rm div}u))\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\lambda\int{\rm div}\dot{u}\left(\alpha\theta^{\alpha-1}{\rm div}u(\theta_{t}+u\cdot\nabla\theta)+\theta^{\alpha}({\rm div}\dot{u}+u\cdot\nabla({\rm div}u)-{\rm div}((u\cdot\nabla)u))\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\lambda\alpha\int\theta^{\alpha-1}{\rm div}\dot{u}{\rm div}u\dot{\theta}{\mathrm{d}}x-\lambda\int\theta^{\alpha}({\rm div}\dot{u})^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\lambda\int\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}\dot{u}\partial_{i}u^{j}\partial_{j}u^{i}{\mathrm{d}}x.

Combining (
3.1.1
) and (
3.1.1
)–(
3.1.1
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int\rho|\dot{u}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{\mu}{4}\left(\frac{\bar{\theta}}{2}\right)^{\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha+2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{4}}^{4}+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-2}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla{\dot{\theta}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha+2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{3}+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-2}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla{\dot{\theta}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(3.52)

Integrating (
3.1.1
) over
(0,\sigma(T))
and using (
3.6
) and (
3.1.1
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{t\in[0,\sigma(T)]}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big|_{t=0}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha+2}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{3}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-2}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla{\dot{\theta}}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha}+C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{3-2\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-2+\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle K_{3}\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha},

(3.53)

provided that
\beta>\alpha>\beta-2
. Then we complete the proof of (
3.46
).

Now we give the proof of (
3.47
). Applying the operator
\partial_{t}+{\rm div}(u\cdot)
to
(\ref{FCNS:2})_{3}
, we get

\displaystyle\rho\partial_{t}\dot{\theta}+\rho u\cdot\nabla\dot{\theta}-\kappa{\rm div}\left(\theta^{\beta}\nabla\dot{\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-R\rho\dot{\theta}{\rm div}u-R\rho\theta{\rm div}\dot{u}+R\rho\theta\partial_{i}u^{j}\partial_{j}u^{i}+\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u\left(2\mu|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}+\lambda({\rm div}u)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\alpha\theta^{\alpha-1}\dot{\theta}\left(2\mu|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}+\lambda({\rm div}u)^{2}\right)+2\mu\theta^{\alpha}\left(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i}\right)\left(\partial_{i}\dot{u}^{j}+\partial_{j}\dot{u}^{i}\right)

\displaystyle-2\mu\theta^{\alpha}\left(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i}\right)\left(\partial_{i}u^{k}\partial_{k}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{k}\partial_{k}u^{i}\right)+2\lambda\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u{\rm div}\dot{u}-2\lambda\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u\partial_{i}u^{j}\partial_{j}u^{i}

\displaystyle+\kappa\beta{\rm div}\left(\theta^{\beta-1}\dot{\theta}\nabla\theta\right)+\kappa{\rm div}\left(u{\rm div}(\theta^{\beta}\nabla\theta)-\theta^{\beta}\nabla(u\cdot\nabla\theta)-\beta\theta^{\beta-1}u\cdot\nabla\theta\nabla\theta\right).

(3.54)

Multiplying
\dot{\theta}
on both sides of (
3.1.1
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int\rho|\dot{\theta}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\kappa\int\theta^{\beta}|\nabla\dot{\theta}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\int\rho|\dot{\theta}|^{2}|\nabla u|{\mathrm{d}}x+C\bar{\theta}\int\rho|\dot{\theta}|\left(|\nabla\dot{u}|+|\nabla u|^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}x+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int|\dot{\theta}||\nabla u|^{3}{\mathrm{d}}x+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\int|\dot{\theta}|^{2}|\nabla u|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int|\dot{\theta}||\nabla u||\nabla\dot{u}|{\mathrm{d}}x+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\int|\nabla{\theta}||\dot{\theta}||\nabla\dot{\theta}|{\mathrm{d}}x+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\int|u||\nabla\theta|^{2}|\nabla\dot{\theta}|{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\int|u||\nabla^{2}{\theta}||\nabla\dot{\theta}|{\mathrm{d}}x+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\int|\nabla u||\nabla{\theta}||\nabla\dot{\theta}|{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle:=~

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{9}N_{i},

(3.55)

where
N_{i}
can be estimated as

\displaystyle N_{1}

\displaystyle=C\int\rho|\dot{\theta}|^{2}|\nabla u|{\mathrm{d}}x\leq C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}\|\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{6}}\leq C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\delta\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\beta}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(3.56)

\displaystyle N_{2}

\displaystyle=C\bar{\theta}\int\rho|\dot{\theta}|\left(|\nabla\dot{u}|+|\nabla u|^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}x\leq C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}+C(K)\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

(3.57)

\displaystyle N_{3}

\displaystyle=C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int|\dot{\theta}||\nabla u|^{3}{\mathrm{d}}x\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{6}}\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\delta\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(K)\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha-\beta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2},

(3.58)

\displaystyle N_{4}

\displaystyle=C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\int|\dot{\theta}|^{2}|\nabla u|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\|\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\leq C(K)\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(3.59)

\displaystyle N_{5}

\displaystyle=C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int|\dot{\theta}||\nabla u||\nabla\dot{u}|{\mathrm{d}}x\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\delta\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha-\beta}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(3.60)

\displaystyle N_{6}

\displaystyle=C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\int|\nabla\dot{\theta}||\nabla\theta||\dot{\theta}|{\mathrm{d}}x\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}\|\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{6}}\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq CK_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(3.61)

\displaystyle N_{7}

\displaystyle=C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\int|u||\nabla\theta|^{2}|\nabla\dot{\theta}|{\mathrm{d}}x\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\delta\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq\delta\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(K)\bar{\theta}^{\beta+1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}},

(3.62)

\displaystyle N_{8}+N_{9}

\displaystyle=C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\int|u||\nabla^{2}{\theta}||\nabla\dot{\theta}|{\mathrm{d}}x+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\int|\nabla u||\nabla{\theta}||\nabla\dot{\theta}|{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\delta\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(3.63)

Taking
\delta=\frac{\kappa}{10\cdot 2^{\beta}}
in (
3.1.1
)–(
3.1.1
) and inserting them into (
3.1.1
), we obtain after integration with respect to
t\in[0,T]
that

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{t\in[0,\sigma(T)]}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big|_{t=0}+C(K)\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\bigg(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\beta}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha-\beta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha-\beta}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\beta+1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg){\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{2\beta}+C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{1+\frac{1}{2}(\alpha+\beta)}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha+\frac{1}{2}\beta}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-\beta}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{3\alpha-\beta}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-\alpha+\frac{3}{2}}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle K_{4}\bar{\theta}^{2\beta},

(3.64)

provided that
\alpha<\beta
. Hence, the proof of Lemma
3.1.1
is completed.
∎

3.1.2
Time-weighted uniform estimates of
(u,\theta)
on
[0,T]

Next, in Lemmas
3.1.2
–
3.1.2
, we will establish the time-weighted uniform estimates of
(u,\theta)
on the time interval
[0,T]
. Moreover, by Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality, we will give the uniform upper and lower bounds of temperature.

Lemma 3.5
.

Under the conditions of Proposition
3
, there exist positive constants
K_{5}
,
K_{6}
and
L_{3}
depending on
\mu
,
\lambda
,
\kappa
, and
R
such that if
(\rho,u,\theta)
is a smooth solution of (
1.15
)–(
1.16
) on
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}\times(0,T]
, it holds

B_{1}(T)=\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{3}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{3}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq K_{5}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta},

(3.65)

B_{2}(T)=\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{4}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq K_{6}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{2},

(3.66)

provided that
2\leq\alpha<\beta<\alpha+2
,
\alpha\leq 10
,
\bar{\theta}\geq L_{3}
,

C(K)\bar{\rho}\leq\min\left\{\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{6}(\alpha+3\beta-4)},\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}(2\alpha+\beta-4)}\right\},

(3.67)

and (
3.20
) holds.

Proof.

First, we claim that

\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\leq 2\bar{\theta},\quad\text{for}~t\in[0,T].

(3.68)

Step 1
: Estimates of
\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}
and
\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}
.

Combining (
3.1.1
), (
3.7
) and (
3.68
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sigma^{5}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\leq~

\displaystyle C\sigma^{5}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\sigma^{4}\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\theta}^{2-2\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(K)\sigma^{5}\bar{\theta}^{-2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{5}{3}\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{2-2\alpha-\beta}+\bar{\theta}^{2-2\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha-\frac{\beta}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{5}{3}\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha-\frac{\beta}{2}}\right),

(3.69)

provided that

C(K)\bar{\rho}\leq\min\left\{\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{6}(\alpha+3\beta-4)},\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}(2\alpha+\beta-4)}\right\}.

By (
3.1.1
), (
3.6
), (
3.7
) and (
3.12
), we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{2-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{-2}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\left(\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{3}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)+C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{2-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{-2}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle CK_{8}\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}.

(3.70)

Moreover, from (
3.6
), (
3.7
), (
3.1.1
), (
3.1.1
), (
3.68
), (
3.1.2
) and (
3.1.2
), we have

\displaystyle\sigma^{6}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-2\beta}\sigma^{6}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-2\beta}\sigma^{3}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{2(\alpha-\beta)}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{3}\right)

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{-2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\left(\sigma^{6}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-2\beta}+\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{3-\alpha-2\beta}\right)+C(K)\bar{\theta}^{2(\alpha-\beta)}\left(\sigma^{3}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\sigma^{5}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\right)^{\frac{9}{10}}

\displaystyle+C(K)\bar{\theta}^{-1}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-2\beta}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{5}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{11}{10}-2\beta}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}+\frac{3}{5}\alpha-\frac{49}{20}\beta}\right)+\frac{\sigma^{6}}{2}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(K)\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-2\beta},

(3.71)

provided that
4\alpha-3\beta\leq 10
, and

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq~

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{T}\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-2\beta}\sigma^{4}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-2\beta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{2(\alpha-\beta)}\sigma^{4}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{3}\right){\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{-2}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle CK_{6}\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-2\beta}+C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{3-\alpha-2\beta}+\bar{\theta}^{\frac{11}{6}-\frac{\alpha}{3}-2\beta}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{9}{4}\beta}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle CK_{6}\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-2\beta}.

(3.72)

Combining (
3.1.1
) and (
3.1.2
), (
3.68
) is proved.

Step 2
: Estimates of
B_{1}(T)
.

Multiplying
(\ref{FCNS:2})_{2}
by
\sigma^{3}\dot{u}
and integrating the resulting equality over
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
yield

\displaystyle\sigma^{3}\int\rho|\dot{u}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x=~

\displaystyle-\sigma^{3}\int\dot{u}\cdot\nabla P{\mathrm{d}}x-2\mu\sigma^{3}\int\theta^{\alpha}\nabla\dot{u}:\mathfrak{D}(u){\mathrm{d}}x-\lambda\sigma^{3}\int\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u{\rm div}\dot{u}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle:=~

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{i=1}^{3}P_{i}.

(3.73)

Noting (
3.22
), we get after integration by parts that

\displaystyle P_{1}=~

\displaystyle R\sigma^{3}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}){\rm div}u_{t}{\mathrm{d}}x+R\sigma^{3}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta})u\cdot\nabla{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x+R\sigma^{3}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta})\partial_{j}u^{i}\partial_{i}u^{j}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle R\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\sigma^{3}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}){\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x\right)-3R\sigma^{2}\sigma^{\prime}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}){\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x-\sigma^{3}\int P_{t}{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+R\sigma^{3}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta})u\cdot\nabla{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x+R\sigma^{3}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta})\partial_{j}u^{i}\partial_{i}u^{j}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle R\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\sigma^{3}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}){\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x\right)-3R\sigma^{2}\sigma^{\prime}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}){\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x+\sigma^{3}\int{\rm div}(Pu){\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-R\sigma^{3}\int\rho\dot{\theta}{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x+R\sigma^{3}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta})u\cdot\nabla{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x+R\sigma^{3}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta})\partial_{j}u^{i}\partial_{i}u^{j}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle R\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\sigma^{3}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}){\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x\right)-3R\sigma^{2}\sigma^{\prime}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}){\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x-R\sigma^{3}\int\rho\dot{\theta}{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+R\sigma^{3}\int\rho\theta\partial_{j}u^{i}\partial_{i}u^{j}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle R\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\sigma^{3}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}){\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x\right)+C\sigma^{2}\sigma^{\prime}\|\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma^{3}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\sigma^{3}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle R\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\sigma^{3}\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}){\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x\right)+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\sigma^{2}\sigma^{\prime}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\sigma^{3}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\sigma^{3}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(3.74)

\displaystyle P_{2}=~

\displaystyle-2\mu\sigma^{3}\int\theta^{\alpha}\nabla u_{t}:\mathfrak{D}(u){\mathrm{d}}x-2\mu\sigma^{3}\int\theta^{\alpha}\nabla((u\cdot\nabla)u):\mathfrak{D}(u){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\mu\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\sigma^{3}\int\theta^{\alpha}|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\right)+2\mu\sigma\sigma^{\prime}\int\theta^{\alpha}|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\alpha\mu\sigma^{3}\int\theta^{\alpha-1}\theta_{t}|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\mu\sigma^{3}\int{\rm div}(\theta^{\alpha}u)|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x-\mu\sigma^{3}\int\theta^{\alpha}\partial_{j}u^{k}\partial_{k}u^{i}\left(\partial_{i}u^{j}+\partial_{j}u^{i}\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle-\mu\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\sigma^{3}\int\theta^{\alpha}|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\right)+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\sigma^{2}\sigma^{\prime}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\sigma^{3}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\sigma^{3}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{3}{2}},

(3.75)

and

\displaystyle P_{3}\leq~

\displaystyle-\frac{\lambda}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\sigma^{3}\int\theta^{\alpha}({\rm div}u)^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\right)+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\sigma^{2}\sigma^{\prime}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\sigma^{3}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\sigma^{3}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{3}{2}}.

(3.76)

Substituting (
3.1.2
)–(
3.1.2
) into (
3.1.2
), we obtain after integration with respect to
t\in[0,T]
that

\displaystyle\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{3}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{3}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\sigma^{3}\int\theta^{\alpha}\left(\mu|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}+\frac{\lambda}{2}({\rm div}u)^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{0}^{T}\int\sigma^{3}\rho|\dot{u}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{3}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\right)+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{3}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{3}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{3}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}+C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}(\alpha-\beta+1)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle K_{5}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta},

(3.77)

provided that
1<\alpha<\beta+1
and

C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}+C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}(\alpha-\beta+1)}\right)\leq K_{5}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}.

Step 3
: Estimates of
B_{2}(T)
.

Multiplying
(\ref{FCNS:2})_{3}
by
\sigma^{4}\theta_{t}
and integrating the resulting equality over
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
lead to

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\sigma^{4}\int\theta^{\beta}|\nabla\theta|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\right)+\sigma^{4}\int\rho|\dot{\theta}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle 2\kappa\sigma^{3}\sigma^{\prime}\int\theta^{\beta}|\nabla\theta|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{\kappa\beta}{2}\sigma^{4}\int\theta^{\beta-1}\theta_{t}|\nabla\theta|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\sigma^{4}\int\rho\dot{\theta}u\cdot\nabla\theta{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-R\sigma^{4}\int\rho\theta{\rm div}u\dot{\theta}{\mathrm{d}}x+R\sigma^{4}\int\rho\theta u\cdot\nabla\theta{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x+2\mu\sigma^{4}\int\theta^{\alpha}\dot{\theta}|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\lambda\sigma^{4}\int\theta^{\alpha}\dot{\theta}({\rm div}u)^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x-2\mu\sigma^{4}\int\theta^{\alpha}u\cdot\nabla\theta|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x-\lambda\sigma^{4}\int\theta^{\alpha}u\cdot\nabla\theta({\rm div}u)^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x.

(3.78)

Similar to (
3.1.1
)–(
3.1.1
), we obtain after integration with respect to
t\in[0,T]
that

\displaystyle\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{4}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\sigma^{4}\int\theta^{\beta}|\nabla\theta|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{2}+C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{3-\alpha-\beta}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{7}{4}+\frac{\alpha}{4}-\frac{\beta}{2}}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{5}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{3-\alpha-\frac{\beta}{2}}+\bar{\theta}^{\frac{5}{2}-\frac{\alpha+\beta}{2}}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{2}}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{1-\frac{3}{4}\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle K_{6}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{2}.

(3.79)

Hence we complete the proof of Lemma
3.1.2
.
∎

The following Lemma is devoted to proving the uniform higher-order time-weighted estimates of velocity and temperature, as well as the uniform estimate of
\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}
.

Lemma 3.6
.

Under the conditions of Proposition
3
, there exist positive constants
K_{7}
and
L_{4}
depending on
\mu
,
\lambda
,
\kappa
, and
R
such that if
(\rho,u,\theta)
is a smooth solution of (
1.15
)–(
1.16
) on
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}\times(0,T]
, it holds that

B_{3}(T)=\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{5}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{5}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq\bar{\theta}^{\frac{\alpha}{3}+\frac{2}{3}},

(3.80)

B_{4}(T)=\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{6}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq K_{7}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{2},

(3.81)

and

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\leq\frac{\bar{\theta}^{2}}{9},

(3.82)

provided that
2\leq\alpha<\beta<\alpha+2
,
\alpha\leq 10
,
\bar{\theta}\geq L_{4}
,

C(K)\bar{\rho}\leq\min\left\{\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}(\alpha-1)},\bar{\theta}\right\},

(3.83)

and (
3.20
), (
3.67
) hold.

Proof.

Step 1
: Estimates of
B_{3}(T)
.

Operating
\sigma^{5}\dot{u}^{j}(\partial_{t}+\mathrm{div}(u\cdot))
to
(\ref{FCNS:2})_{2}^{j}
, summing with respect to
j
, and integrating the resulting equation over
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int\sigma^{5}\rho|\dot{u}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x-\frac{5}{2}\sigma^{4}\sigma^{\prime}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\int\sigma^{5}\dot{u}^{j}\left(\partial_{j}P_{t}+{\rm div}(u\partial_{j}P)\right){\mathrm{d}}x+2\mu\int\sigma^{5}\dot{u}^{j}\left(\partial_{t}({\rm div}(\theta^{\alpha}\mathfrak{D}(u)))+{\rm div}(u{\rm div}(\theta^{\alpha}\mathfrak{D}(u)))\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\lambda\int\sigma^{5}\dot{u}^{j}\left(\partial_{t}(\partial_{j}(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u))+{\rm div}(u\cdot\partial_{j}(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u))\right){\mathrm{d}}x.

(3.84)

Similar to (
3.1.1
)–(
3.1.1
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int\sigma^{5}\rho|\dot{u}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{\mu\sigma^{5}}{4}\left(\frac{\bar{\theta}}{2}\right)^{\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{5}{2}\sigma^{4}\sigma^{\prime}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\sigma^{5}\Big(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{3}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big).

(3.85)

Integrating (
3.1.2
) over
[0,T]
, we have

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{5}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{5}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\sigma^{4}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{5}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-2}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{3}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{5}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{3-2\alpha}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{\alpha}{3}-\frac{11}{15}}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{6}{5}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{5}\alpha-\frac{\beta}{5}-1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\bar{\theta}^{\frac{\alpha}{3}+\frac{2}{3}},

(3.86)

provided that
4\alpha-3\beta<25
and

C(K)\bar{\rho}\leq\min\left\{\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}(\alpha-1)},\bar{\theta}\right\}.

Step 2
: Estimates of
B_{4}(T)
.

Multiplying
\sigma^{6}\dot{\theta}
on both sides of (
3.1.1
) and integrating the resulting equality over
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
, we get

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{6}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\sigma^{5}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int\rho|\dot{\theta}|^{2}|\nabla u|{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int\rho|\dot{\theta}|\left(|\nabla\dot{u}|+|\nabla u|^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int|\dot{\theta}||\nabla u|^{3}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int|\dot{\theta}|^{2}|\nabla u|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int|\dot{\theta}||\nabla\dot{u}||\nabla u|{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int|\dot{\theta}||\nabla\dot{\theta}||\nabla\theta|{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int|\nabla\dot{\theta}|\left(|u||\nabla^{2}\theta|+|\nabla u||\nabla\theta|\right){\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int|\nabla\dot{\theta}||u||\nabla\theta|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle:=~

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\sigma^{5}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+\sum\limits_{i=1}^{8}Q_{i},

(3.87)

where the terms
Q_{i}
can be estimated as follows.

\displaystyle Q_{1}=~

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int\rho|\dot{\theta}|^{2}|\nabla u|{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t\leq C\bar{\rho}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{\theta}^{\beta}}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{-3\beta}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{6}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{4}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{\theta}^{\beta}}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{5}\bar{\theta}^{2-2\alpha-2\beta},

(3.88)

\displaystyle Q_{2}=~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int\rho|\dot{\theta}|\left(|\nabla\dot{u}|+|\nabla u|^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{4}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{2}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(K)\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{7}{3}-\frac{\alpha}{3}}+C(K)\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{11}{4}-\frac{3}{4}\alpha},

(3.89)

\displaystyle Q_{3}=~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int|\dot{\theta}||\nabla u|^{3}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{\theta}^{\beta}}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha-\beta}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{6}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{\theta}^{\beta}}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C(K)\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{2-\beta},

(3.90)

\displaystyle Q_{4}=~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int|\dot{\theta}|^{2}|\nabla u|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq\frac{\bar{\theta}^{\beta}}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t,

(3.91)

\displaystyle Q_{5}=~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int|\dot{\theta}||\nabla\dot{u}||\nabla u|{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{\theta}^{\beta}}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha-\beta}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{5}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{\theta}^{\beta}}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C(K)\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha+1-\beta},

(3.92)

\displaystyle Q_{6}=~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int|\dot{\theta}||\nabla\dot{\theta}||\nabla\theta|{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq\frac{\bar{\theta}^{\beta}}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t,

(3.93)

\displaystyle Q_{7}=~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int|\nabla\dot{\theta}|\left(|u||\nabla^{2}\theta|+|\nabla u||\nabla\theta|\right){\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{\theta}^{\beta}}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{\theta}^{\beta}}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{2-\beta},

(3.94)

\displaystyle Q_{8}=~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\int|\nabla\dot{\theta}||u||\nabla\theta|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x{\mathrm{d}}t\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{\theta}^{\beta}}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-2}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\sigma^{6}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{4}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{\bar{\theta}^{\beta}}{8}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{6}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{5}\bar{\theta}^{3-\alpha-\beta}.

(3.95)

Inserting (
3.1.2
)–(
3.1.2
) into (
3.1.2
), then taking
\bar{\theta}\geq L_{4}
and
K_{7}\geq CK_{6}
, we obtain

B_{4}(T)\leq CK_{6}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{2}\leq K_{7}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{2}.

Step 3
: Estimates of
\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}
.

At last, from (
3.1.1
) and (
3.1.2
), we have

\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq 2\bar{\theta}^{2}.

(3.96)

From (
3.19
) and (
3.66
), we have

\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C(K).

(3.97)

Hence we get

\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\leq C\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\leq C(K)\bar{\theta}\leq\frac{\bar{\theta}^{2}}{9},

(3.98)

provided that
\bar{\theta}\geq L_{4}
. Thus we complete the proof.
∎

3.1.3
Uniform estimates of density on
[0,T]

Next, we will establish the first-order uniform estimates of density, which is achieved by combining the estimates on
[0,\sigma(T)]
and the time-weighted estimate on
[0,T]
. As a result, the uniform lower and upper bounds of density is obtained by Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality.

Lemma 3.7
.

Under the conditions of Proposition
3
, there exist positive constants
\widetilde{L}
,
L_{5}
,
K_{8}
and
K_{9}
depending on
\mu
,
\lambda
,
\kappa
, and
R
, such that if
(\rho,u,\theta)
is a smooth solution of (
1.15
)–(
1.16
) on
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}\times(0,T]
, it holds that

A_{5}(T)=\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq K_{8},

(3.99)

A_{6}(T)=\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq K_{9},

(3.100)

and

\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\frac{\bar{\rho}}{3},

(3.101)

provided that
\bar{\rho}\geq\widetilde{L}
,
\bar{\theta}\geq L_{5}
,
C(K)\bar{\rho}\leq\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{23}(7\alpha-1)}
, and (
3.20
), (
3.67
), (
3.83
) hold.

Proof.

Applying
\nabla
operator on
(\ref{FCNS:2})_{1}
and multiplying
\nabla\rho|\nabla\rho|^{r-2}
on both sides of the resulting equation, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{r}\left(|\nabla\rho|^{r}\right)_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{r}{\rm div}(|\nabla\rho|^{r}u)+\frac{r-1}{r}|\nabla\rho|^{r}{\rm div}u+|\nabla\rho|^{r-2}(\nabla\rho)^{\top}\nabla u\nabla\rho

\displaystyle+\frac{\rho}{2\mu+\lambda}|\nabla\rho|^{r-2}\nabla\rho\cdot\left(\nabla G+R\theta^{1-\alpha}\nabla\rho+R\rho\theta^{-\alpha}\nabla\theta\right)=0.

(3.102)

Integrating the above equation in
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{r}}^{r}+\frac{R}{2\mu+\lambda}\int\rho{\theta}^{1-\alpha}|\nabla\rho|^{r}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{r}}^{r}+C\|\nabla G\|_{L^{r}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{r}}^{r-1}+C\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{r}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{r}}^{r-1}.

(3.103)

Multiplying (
3.1.3
) by
\sigma^{m}
with
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\sigma^{m}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{r}}^{r}\right)+\frac{R\sigma^{m}}{2\mu+\lambda}\int\rho{\theta}^{1-\alpha}|\nabla\rho|^{r}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle m\sigma^{m-1}\sigma^{\prime}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{r}}^{r}+C\sigma^{m}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{r}}^{r}+C\sigma^{m}\|\nabla G\|_{L^{r}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{r}}^{r-1}+C\sigma^{m}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{r}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{r}}^{r-1}.

(3.104)

Case 1
: When
r=2
, (
3.1.3
) reduces to

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{c_{0}}{4}\bar{\rho}\left(2\bar{\theta}\right)^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla G\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}}{8}\bar{\rho}\left(2\bar{\theta}\right)^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla G\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{-1-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}}{8}\bar{\rho}\left(2\bar{\theta}\right)^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla G\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{-1-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(3.105)

for some positive constant
c_{0}
depending on
\lambda
,
\mu
and
R
.

By (
2.6
), we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla G\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|H\|_{L^{2}}\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\right).

(3.106)

Inserting (
3.1.3
) into (
3.1.3
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{c_{0}}{4}\bar{\rho}\left(2\bar{\theta}\right)^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{-1-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}}{8}\bar{\rho}\left(2\bar{\theta}\right)^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-1-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{-1-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-3}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{12}{7}}.

(3.107)

It remains to estimate
\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}
on the right hand side of (
3.1.3
). From
(\ref{FCNS:2})_{2}
, one has

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\left(\bar{\rho}\|\dot{u}\|_{L^{4}}+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla\theta\nabla u\|_{L^{4}}+\bar{\theta}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+\bar{\rho}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\left(\bar{\rho}\|\dot{u}\|_{L^{4}}+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}+\bar{\theta}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+\bar{\rho}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\left(\bar{\rho}\|\dot{u}\|_{L^{4}}+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}+\bar{\theta}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+\bar{\rho}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\dot{u}\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}+C\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\dot{u}\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-7}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}^{7}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\frac{1}{4}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}},

(3.108)

which implies

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}\leq C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\dot{u}\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-7}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}^{7}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}.

(3.109)

By using (
3.109
)
and integrating with respect to
t
over
[0,\sigma(T)]
,
we arrive at

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\dot{u}\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{-14}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}^{14}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{2-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\rho\|^{2}_{L^{4}}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle:=~

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{i=1}^{4}R_{i},

(3.110)

where, from (
3.6
), we have

\displaystyle R_{1}=~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\dot{u}\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq~C(K)\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}.

(3.111)

By (
3.6
) and (
3.7
), we have

\displaystyle R_{2}

\displaystyle=C\bar{\theta}^{-14}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}^{14}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq C\bar{\theta}^{-14}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,\sigma(T)]}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}^{14}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-13-\alpha}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,\sigma(T)]}\left(\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{7}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{21}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C(K)\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{5}{2}-\alpha},

(3.112)

\displaystyle R_{3}=~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{2-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\rho\|^{2}_{L^{4}}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq CK_{9}\bar{\theta}^{2-2\alpha},

(3.113)

and

\displaystyle R_{4}

\displaystyle=C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\left(\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq C(K)\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}-2\alpha-\beta}.

(3.114)

Now substituting (
3.111
)–(
3.1.3
) into (
3.1.3
), one can see that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq C(K)\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}.

(3.115)

Next, we will estimate
\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}
in two cases.

Case 1.1
: For the case
t\in(0,\sigma(T))
, integrating (
3.1.3
) over
(0,\sigma(T))
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\rho_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-1-\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{-1-\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-3}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{12}{7}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\rho_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha-\beta}+CK_{1}\bar{\theta}^{-1}+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{7}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{20}{7}}+C\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t,

(3.116)

where we have used (
3.6
).

By Gronwall’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\|\nabla\rho_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha-\beta}+CK_{1}\bar{\theta}^{-1}+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{7}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{20}{7}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\exp\left\{\left(\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{14}}\left(\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{3}{7}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\exp\left\{C(K)\bar{\rho}^{\frac{23}{14}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{14}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C,

(3.117)

provided that
C(K)\bar{\rho}\leq\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{23}(7\alpha-1)}
, where we have used (
3.6
) and (
3.115
).

Case 1.2
: For the case
t\in(\sigma(T),T)
, by (
3.109
) and (
3.7
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{5}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{5}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\theta}^{-14}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}^{14}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{2-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\rho\|^{2}_{L^{4}}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{3}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{5}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{-14}\left(\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}^{14}\right)\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{2-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\rho\|^{2}_{L^{4}}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(K_{5}^{\frac{1}{4}}\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{4}-\frac{7}{4}\alpha}+K_{2}^{\frac{7}{4}}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{5}{2}-\alpha}+K_{9}\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}+K_{6}^{\frac{3}{4}}\bar{\rho}^{4}\bar{\theta}^{2-2\alpha-\frac{7}{4}\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle CK_{9}\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}.

(3.118)

When
r=2
, (
3.1.3
) reduces to

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\sigma^{m}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{c_{0}}{4}\sigma^{m}\bar{\rho}\left(2\bar{\theta}\right)^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle m\sigma^{m-1}\sigma^{\prime}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\sigma^{m}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\sigma^{m}\|\nabla G\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\sigma^{m}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle m\sigma^{m-1}\sigma^{\prime}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\sigma^{m}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\sigma^{m}\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\sigma^{m}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle m\sigma^{m-1}\sigma^{\prime}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\sigma^{m}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\sigma^{m}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\sigma^{m}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-1}\sigma^{m}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}},

(3.119)

where we have used (
3.1.3
).

Taking
