and integrating (
3.1.3
) over
[0,T]
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sigma^{3}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{3}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\sup\limits_{t\in[0,\sigma(T)]}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{14}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{5}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{3}{7}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{3}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+C\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{14}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{5}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{3}{7}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}K_{8}+C\left(K_{8}\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{6}{7}}+K_{5}^{\frac{1}{2}}K_{8}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha}+K_{8}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha-\frac{1}{2}\beta}+K_{8}^{\frac{1}{2}}K_{9}^{\frac{3}{7}}\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{7}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha-\frac{1}{2}\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4}K_{8},

(3.120)

where we have used (
3.1.3
), (
3.7
), (
3.7
) and (
3.1.3
), and
taking

C\left(K_{8}\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{6}{7}}+K_{5}^{\frac{1}{2}}K_{8}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha}+K_{8}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha-\frac{1}{2}\beta}+K_{8}^{\frac{1}{2}}K_{9}^{\frac{3}{7}}\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{7}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha-\frac{1}{2}\beta}\right)\leq\frac{1}{4}K_{8}.

Combining (
3.1.3
) and (
3.1.3
), we complete the proof of (
3.99
).

Case 2
: When
r=4
, (
3.1.3
) reduces to

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{4}+\frac{R}{2\mu+\lambda}\int\rho{\theta}^{1-\alpha}|\nabla\rho|^{4}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{4}+C\|\nabla G\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{3}+C\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{3}.

(3.121)

By (
2.6
), we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla G\|_{L^{4}}\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\dot{u}\|_{L^{4}}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\right).

(3.122)

Substituting (
3.1.3
) into (
3.1.3
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\frac{c_{0}}{4}\bar{\rho}\left(2\bar{\theta}\right)^{1-\alpha}\int|\nabla\rho|^{4}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C\|\nabla G\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}}{8}\bar{\rho}\left(2\bar{\theta}\right)^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C\|\nabla G\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{-1-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}}{8}\bar{\rho}\left(2\bar{\theta}\right)^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\right)\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{-1-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}^{2}.

(3.123)

Then, similar as the estimates of
\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}
.

Case 2.1
: For the case
t\in(0,\sigma(T))
, integrating (
3.1.3
) over
(0,\sigma(T))
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\rho_{0}\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\right)\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{-1-\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C\|\nabla\rho_{0}\|_{L^{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,\sigma(T)]}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}\left(\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{8}}\left(\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{3}{8}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-1-\alpha}\left(\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\left(\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{-1}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+\|\nabla\rho_{0}\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+CK_{1}^{\frac{1}{8}}K_{3}^{\frac{3}{8}}K_{9}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha}+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}-\alpha-\beta}+C(K)\bar{\rho}^{\frac{5}{7}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{5}{28}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha}

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t,

(3.124)

where we have used (
3.6
).

By Gronwall’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{t\in[0,\sigma(T)]}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\|\nabla\rho_{0}\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C(K)\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{2}-\alpha-\beta}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{5}{7}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{5}{28}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha}\right)\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\exp\left\{\left(\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{14}}\left(\int_{0}^{\sigma(T)}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{3}{7}}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\exp\left\{C(K)\bar{\rho}^{\frac{23}{14}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{14}-\frac{7}{14}\alpha}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C,

(3.125)

Case 2.2
: Multiplying (
3.1.3
) by
\sigma^{m}
with
m\in\mathbb{N}_{+}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\sigma^{m}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\right)+\frac{c_{0}}{4}\sigma^{m}\bar{\rho}\left(2\bar{\theta}\right)^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle m\sigma^{m-1}\sigma^{\prime}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C\sigma^{m}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C\sigma^{m}\|\nabla G\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\sigma^{m}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle m\sigma^{m-1}\sigma^{\prime}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C\sigma^{m}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\sigma^{m}\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\right)\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\sigma^{m}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle m\sigma^{m-1}\sigma^{\prime}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C\sigma^{m}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\sigma^{m}\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\right)\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}},

(3.126)

where we have used (
3.1.3
).

Taking
m=4
and integrating (
3.1.3
) over
[0,T]
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sigma^{4}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\sup\limits_{t\in[0,\sigma(T)]}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{14}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{3}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{3}{7}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{3}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{8}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{5}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{3}{8}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{8}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{3}{8}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{-1}\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{8}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{4}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{3}{8}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{1}{14}}\left(\int_{0}^{T}\sigma^{5}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\right)^{\frac{3}{7}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}K_{9}+C\left(K_{9}^{\frac{13}{14}}\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{6}{7}}+K_{5}^{\frac{1}{8}}\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{3}{4}\alpha-\frac{1}{4}}+K_{6}^{\frac{3}{8}}\bar{\rho}^{\frac{11}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha-\frac{7}{8}\beta}+K_{6}^{\frac{3}{8}}K_{9}^{\frac{13}{14}}\bar{\rho}^{\frac{4}{7}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha-\frac{7}{8}\beta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4}K_{9},

(3.127)

where we have used (
3.1.3
), (
3.7
) and (
3.1.3
), provided that

C\left(K_{9}^{\frac{13}{14}}\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{6}{7}}+K_{5}^{\frac{1}{8}}\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{3}{4}\alpha-\frac{1}{4}}+K_{6}^{\frac{3}{8}}\bar{\rho}^{\frac{11}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha-\frac{7}{8}\beta}+K_{6}^{\frac{3}{8}}K_{9}^{\frac{13}{14}}\bar{\rho}^{\frac{4}{7}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{2}-\frac{1}{2}\alpha-\frac{7}{8}\beta}\right)\leq\frac{1}{4}K_{9}.

Combining (
3.1.3
) and (
3.1.3
), we have proved (
3.100
). From (
3.99
) and (
3.100
), we get

\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\leq C\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{4}{3}}\leq C(K)\leq\frac{1}{3}\bar{\rho}.

(3.128)

This completes the proof.
∎

Finally, taking

L\geq\max\{L_{i}\}_{i=1}^{5},

we complete the proof of Proposition
3
from Lemmas
3.1.1
–
3.1.3
.

3.2
Higher-order estimates

In this subsection, we will give the uniform estimate of the solution in
H^{2}
space.

Lemma 3.8
.

Under the conditions of Proposition
3
, it holds that

\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\|u\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\right)+\int_{0}^{T}\left(\|u_{t}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\theta_{t}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}t\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\beta},

(3.129)

\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\|\rho_{t}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\right)\leq C\bar{\theta}^{2},

(3.130)

and

\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\left(\|\nabla\rho_{t}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{2}{7}}\exp\left\{C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{2}{7}}\right\}.

(3.131)

Proof.

First, the following estimates come from Proposition
3
and Lemma
3.1
directly:

\displaystyle\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{H^{2}}^{2}\leq C\bar{\theta}^{2}.

(3.132)

From
(\ref{FCNS:2})_{2}
, one has

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\left(\bar{\rho}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{2}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha-2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha}+\bar{\theta}^{2}+\bar{\rho}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{2\alpha-2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}+C\bar{\theta}^{2-2\alpha}+C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}+C\bar{\theta}^{-2}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}+C\bar{\theta}^{-2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho},

(3.133)

which implies

\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\bar{\rho}.

It remains to estimate

\|\rho_{t}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{T}\left(\|u_{t}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\theta_{t}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}t.

We divide the proof into several steps.

Step 1
: Estimate of
u_{t}
and
\theta_{t}
in
L^{2}\left(0,T;H^{1}\right)
.

From (
3.8
) and (
3.9
), one has

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\left(\|u_{t}+u\cdot\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla u_{t}+\nabla(u\cdot\nabla u)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}t\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}.

(3.134)

Therefore, by (
3.8
), (
3.1.1
), and (
3.9
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\|u_{t}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}+C\int_{0}^{T}\left(\|u\cdot\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{4}}^{4}+\|u\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}+C\int_{0}^{T}\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{3}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{3}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}+C\int_{0}^{T}\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}t.

(3.135)

From (
3.2
), one has

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{-2\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\left(\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{2}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{-2}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha},

(3.136)

where we have used (
3.8
), (
3.9
), (
3.1.2
), (
3.12
), and (
3.1.2
).

Inserting (
3.12
), (
3.1.2
) and (
3.2
) into (
3.2
), we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\|u_{t}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}.

(3.137)

From (
3.8
) and (
3.9
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\left(\|\theta_{t}+u\cdot\nabla\theta\|^{2}+\|\nabla\theta_{t}+\nabla(u\cdot\nabla\theta)\|^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}t\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}.

(3.138)

Therefore, by (
3.8
), (
3.1.1
), and (
3.9
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\|\theta_{t}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}+C\int_{0}^{T}\left(\|u\cdot\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\cdot\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}+C\int_{0}^{T}\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}+C\bar{\rho}\int_{0}^{T}\left(\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}t.

(3.139)

Inserting (
3.12
), (
3.1.1
) and (
3.1.2
) into (
3.2
), we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T}\|\theta_{t}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}.

(3.140)

Step 2
: Estimate of
\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
and
\|\rho_{t}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}
.

By (
1.15
)
1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\rho_{t}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|{u}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\bar{\rho},

(3.141)

and

\displaystyle\nabla\rho_{t}+(u\cdot\nabla)\nabla\rho+\nabla u\nabla\rho+\nabla\rho\text{div}u+\rho\nabla\text{div}u=0.

(3.142)

Then (
3.142
) implies

\displaystyle\|\nabla\rho_{t}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\|{u}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla{u}\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(3.143)

By direct computation, we derive the equation of
\nabla^{2}\rho
as follows:

\displaystyle(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{t}+2(\nabla u\cdot\nabla)\nabla\rho+(u\cdot\nabla)\nabla^{2}\rho+\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla^{2}u+\nabla^{2}\rho\text{div}u+2\nabla\rho\otimes\nabla\text{div}u+\rho\nabla^{2}\text{div}u=0.

Multiplying the above equation by
\nabla^{2}\rho
and integrating the resulting equation over
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+c_{0}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}\|\nabla^{2}G\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{-(\alpha+1)}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}}{2}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{12}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla H\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{-(\alpha+1)}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}}{2}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{12}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{-(\alpha+1)}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{-(\alpha+1)}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{-(\alpha+1)}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-(\alpha+1)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{12}{7}},

(3.144)

where we have used

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}G\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\nabla H\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-(\alpha+1)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-(\alpha+1)}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{1}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}+C\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{1}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+C\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}+C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}.

(3.145)

Then, integrating (
3.2
) over
(0,T)
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}\rho_{0}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{12}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{-(\alpha+1)}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}^{3}\bar{\theta}^{-(\alpha+1)}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{-(\alpha+1)}\int_{0}^{T}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-(\alpha+1)}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{12}{7}}{\mathrm{d}}t

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{2}{7}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{12}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t,

(3.146)

then, by using Gronwall’s inequality and (
3.1.3
), we have

\displaystyle\sup\limits_{t\in[0,T]}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{2}{7}}\exp\left\{C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\int_{0}^{T}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{12}{7}}{\mathrm{d}}t\right\}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{2}{7}}\exp\left\{C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{2}{7}}\right\},

(3.147)

where we have used (
3.46
), (
3.1.3
), (
3.115
), (
3.12
), and (
3.1.2
).

Combining (
3.132
), (
3.137
), (
3.140
), (
3.2
), and (
3.143
), we complete the proof.
∎

4
Decay estimate of the solution

The aim of this section is to acquire the convergence rate of the global solution
(\rho,u,\theta)
obtained in Theorem
1.1
to the equilibrium
(\bar{\rho},0,\bar{\theta})
. For simplicity, we use
C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})
to denote a generic positive constant that depends on
\bar{\rho}
,
\bar{\theta}
,
\mu
,
\lambda
,
\kappa
,
R
,
K_{i}
and the initial data. The following lemma is devoted to giving the large time behaviour of the strong solution
(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)
in
H^{1}
space.

Lemma 4.1
.

Let
(\rho,u,\theta)
be the global strong solution obtained in Theorem
1.1
. Then, we have

\lim_{t\rightarrow\infty}\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}\right)=0.

(4.1)

Proof.

From Lemma
3.1
, we have

\int_{0}^{\infty}\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\mathrm{d}t\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta}).

(4.2)

It follows from (
4.2
) and Lemma
3.1.2
that

\int_{0}^{\infty}\left|\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right|\mathrm{d}t\leq\int_{0}^{\infty}\int|\nabla u\cdot\nabla u_{t}|\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\leq\int_{0}^{\infty}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u_{t}\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}t\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta}).

(4.3)

Combining (
4.2
) and (
4.3
) gives that
\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow\infty}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}=0
. Integrating (
3.1.2
) with respect to
t\in[0,\infty)
, we can obtain that

\int_{0}^{\infty}\left|\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right|\mathrm{d}t\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta}).

(4.4)

Combining (
4.2
) and (
4.4
) gives that
\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow\infty}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}=0
. Similarly, from (
3.99
), (
3.1.3
), (
3.2
) and (
3.2
), one has
\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow\infty}\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}=0
.

Now it remains to prove that
\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow\infty}(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}})=0.
It follows from (
3.18
) and (
3.65
) that

\int_{0}^{\infty}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}t\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta}).

(4.5)

Besides, from (
3.1.1
), we have

\int_{0}^{\infty}\left|\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right|\mathrm{d}t\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta}),

(4.6)

which, along with (
4.5
), yields that
\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow\infty}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=0
. From (
3.2
), we have

\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta}),

(4.7)

which implies that
\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow\infty}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}=0
.

It follows from (
3.19
) and (
3.66
) that

\int_{0}^{\infty}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\mathrm{d}t\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta}).

(4.8)

From (
3.9
), we have

\int_{0}^{\infty}\left|\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right|\mathrm{d}t\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta}),

(4.9)

which, along with (
4.8
), yields
\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow\infty}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=0
. Then from (
3.1.1
) and (
3.1.2
), we have
\lim\limits_{t\rightarrow\infty}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}=0
. Thus we complete the proof of the lemma.
∎

4.1
Decay rate of
\|(\rho-\bar{\rho},u,\theta-\bar{\theta})\|_{L^{2}}

First, we define the total energy as follows.

\displaystyle X(t):=~

\displaystyle\int\left(\frac{1}{2}\rho|u|^{2}+R\bar{\theta}(\rho\ln\rho-\rho-\rho\ln\bar{\rho}+\bar{\rho})+\rho(\theta-\bar{\theta}\ln\theta-\bar{\theta}+\bar{\theta}\ln\bar{\theta})\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\mu\int\theta^{\alpha}|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{\lambda}{2}\int\theta^{\alpha}({\rm div}u)^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x-R\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}){\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{\kappa}{2}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{3}}\int\theta^{\beta}|\nabla\theta|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\bar{\theta}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.10)

The following lemma is devoted to deriving a dissipation inequality of
(\rho,u,\theta)
, which will play a key role in the proof of (
1.21
).

Lemma 4.2
.

Let
(\rho,u,\theta)
be the global solution of (
1.15
) obtained in Theorem
1.1
, then we have

\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}X(t)+c_{1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla u,\nabla\theta,\nabla\rho)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\|(\nabla\dot{u},\nabla\dot{\theta})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq 0,

(4.11)

for any
t\geq 1
.

Proof.

By (
3.1
) and (
3.9
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int\left(\frac{1}{2}\rho|u|^{2}+R\bar{\theta}(\rho\ln\rho-\rho-\rho\ln\bar{\rho}+\bar{\rho})+\rho(\theta-\bar{\theta}\ln\theta-\bar{\theta}+\bar{\theta}\ln\bar{\theta})\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+c_{2}\left(\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq 0.

(4.12)

From (
3.1.2
)–(
3.1.2
) and (
3.1.2
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\mu\int\theta^{\alpha}|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{\lambda}{2}\int\theta^{\alpha}({\rm div}u)^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x-R\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}){\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x\right){\mathrm{d}}x+\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{5}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{3}{8}\alpha-\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{\alpha}{4}+\frac{1}{6}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{\alpha}{4}-\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\theta}^{4-2\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{4-4\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{4-4\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{\alpha}{4}-\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\theta}^{6-4\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(4.13)

where we have used

\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\leq C\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{5}{3}\alpha}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha-\frac{\beta}{2}}\right)\leq C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{3}{2}\alpha}.

(4.14)

From (
3.1.2
), (
3.66
), (
3.1.2
) and (
4.14
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\frac{\kappa}{2}\int\theta^{\beta}|\nabla\theta|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\right)+\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{\beta}{4}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{9}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{5}{8}\alpha-\frac{\beta}{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{9}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{5}{8}\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{13}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{4}{3}-\frac{5}{8}\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}+\frac{3}{8}\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{5}{8}\alpha-\frac{\beta}{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{5}{8}\alpha-\frac{\beta}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{5}{4}\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{4}{3}-\frac{5}{8}\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{4}{3}-\frac{5}{8}\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{2}{3}+\frac{3}{4}\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{3}{2}\beta}\right)\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\left(\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{5}{8}\alpha-\frac{\beta}{2}}+\bar{\theta}^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{5}{4}\alpha}+\bar{\theta}^{\frac{4}{3}-\frac{5}{8}\alpha}+\bar{\theta}^{\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}+\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{3}-\frac{\alpha}{4}}\right)\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{5}{4}\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{8}{3}-\frac{5}{4}\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{2}{3}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{4}{3}-\frac{\alpha}{4}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{5}{4}\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{8}{3}-\frac{5}{4}\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.15)

From (
3.109
), (
3.66
) and (
3.1.2
), we have

\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}\leq C\left(\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}+\bar{\rho}^{7}\bar{\theta}^{-6\beta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}+\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\right).

(4.16)

From (
3.1.3
), (
3.1.3
), (
4.16
), (
3.66
), (
3.1.2
) and (
4.14
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+c_{3}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{6}{7}\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{14}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{9}{14}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{6}\bar{\theta}^{-5\beta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\frac{6}{7}(1-\alpha)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{6}{7}\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{14}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{9}{14}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\right)+\frac{c_{2}}{10}\left(\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\beta-2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{10}\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{3}}\bar{\theta}^{1-\frac{4}{3}\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{3}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{4}{3}\alpha-\frac{\beta}{3}-\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\right)+\frac{c_{2}}{10}\left(\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\beta-2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{10}\left(\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{4}{3}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{3}}\bar{\theta}^{1-\frac{4}{3}\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\rho}^{4}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{7}{12}\alpha-\frac{7}{3}\beta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\right)+\frac{c_{2}}{10}\left(\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\beta-2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{10}\left(\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{4}{3}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{3}}\bar{\theta}^{1-\frac{4}{3}\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(4.17)

and

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\right)+c_{3}\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{6}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{6}{7}\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{14}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{9}{14}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\rho}^{6}\bar{\theta}^{-5\beta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\frac{6}{7}(1-\alpha)}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{13}{7}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{3}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{6}{7}\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{7}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{14}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{9}{14}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{8}}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\frac{c_{2}}{10}\left(\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\beta-2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{10}\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{3}}\bar{\theta}^{1-\frac{4}{3}\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{4}{3}\alpha-\frac{\beta}{3}-\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\frac{c_{2}}{10}\left(\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\beta-2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{10}\left(\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{4}{3}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{3}}\bar{\theta}^{1-\frac{4}{3}\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{7}{12}\alpha-\frac{7}{3}\beta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}^{1-\alpha}\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\right)+\frac{c_{2}}{10}\left(\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\beta-2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{10}\left(\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{4}{3}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C\bar{\rho}^{\frac{7}{3}}\bar{\theta}^{1-\frac{4}{3}\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.18)

After (
4.1
)
+
(
4.1
)
+~\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{3}}\times
(

)
+~\bar{\theta}\times
(

)
+~\bar{\theta}\times

), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int\left(\frac{1}{2}\rho|u|^{2}+R\bar{\theta}(\rho\ln\rho-\rho-\rho\ln\bar{\rho}+\bar{\rho})+\rho(\theta-\bar{\theta}\ln\theta-\bar{\theta}+\bar{\theta}\ln\bar{\theta})\right){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\mu\int\theta^{\alpha}|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{\lambda}{2}\int\theta^{\alpha}({\rm div}u)^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x-R\int(\rho\theta-\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}){\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\frac{\kappa}{2}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{3}}\int\theta^{\beta}|\nabla\theta|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x\right)+\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(\bar{\theta}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+c_{4}\left(\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{2-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{2-\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{2-\frac{4}{3}\alpha}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{\alpha}{4}-\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.19)

Next, we will derive the estimate of
(\dot{u},\dot{\theta})
. From (

), (

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}+\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{3}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{7}{3}-\frac{\alpha}{2}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}+\bar{\rho}^{\frac{5}{4}}\bar{\theta}^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{\alpha}{4}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}^{2}\bar{\theta}^{2-\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho})\left(\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-\frac{14}{3}}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-\alpha}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{1-\frac{5}{4}\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.20)

From (
3.1.1
)–(
3.1.1
), (

), (

) and (
3.1.2
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\kappa}{2}\left(\frac{\bar{\theta}}{2}\right)^{\beta}\|\nabla\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{-\beta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{3}+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho})\left(\bar{\theta}^{\frac{2}{3}-\frac{5}{4}\alpha-\beta}+1\right)\|\sqrt{\rho}\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\left(\bar{\theta}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{\frac{2}{3}+\frac{3}{4}\alpha-\beta}\right)\|\nabla\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho})\bar{\theta}^{\frac{7}{3}-\frac{3}{2}\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq~

(4.21)

) and (

) to

\displaystyle\big\|\widehat{f}(\xi)\big\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\|f(x)\|_{L^{1}},

451.29065pt}\|_{L^{p_{0}^{\prime}}}^{2}\right)\left(\int_{S(t)}\,{\mathrm{d}}\xi\right)^{1-\frac{2}{p_{0}^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\bar{\theta}\left(\|\rho_{0}-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{p_{0}}}^{2}+\|\rho_{0}u_{0}\|_{L^{p_{0}}}^{2}+\|\rho_{0}(\theta_{0}-\bar{\theta})\|_{L^{p_{0}}}^{2}\right)(1+t)^{-2\beta({p_{0}})},

(4.34)

due to
\rho_{0}-\bar{\rho}
,
\rho_{0}u_{0}
,
\rho_{0}(\theta_{0}-\bar{\theta})
belong to
L^{p_{0}}
for
\displaystyle 1\leq{p_{0}}<2
and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{0}}+\frac{1}{p_{0}^{\prime}}=1
.
∎

Now we are in a position to prove (
1.21
). In fact, we will prove the following decay estimate.

Lemma 4.4
.

Suppose the condition of Theorem
1.1
and Theorem
1.2
hold. Let
(\rho,u,\theta)
be the global strong solution obtained in Theorem
1.1
. Then we have

\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{H^{1}}+\|u\|_{H^{1}}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+\|(\dot{u},\dot{\theta})\|_{L^{2}}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1)}.

(4.35)

Proof.

We separate the proof into several steps.

Step 1
: Due to fact
(\rho-\bar{\rho},u,\theta-\bar{\theta})\in L^{\infty}(0,\infty;H^{1})
and
(\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\in L^{2}(0,\infty;L^{2})
, we conclude from (
4.3
) that

\displaystyle\int_{S(t)}\left(R\bar{\theta}|\widehat{\rho-\bar{\rho}}(t)|^{2}+|\widehat{\rho u}(t)|^{2}+|\hbox{\hskip \displaystyle\leq~

\rho\dot{u}=-\nabla P+2\mu{\rm div}(\theta^{\alpha}\mathfrak{D}(u))+\lambda\nabla(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u)

tyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.7pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-451.29065pt\hskip 451.29065pt}\right|^{2}+\left|\hbox{\hskip 451.29065pt\hskip-20.9964pt\hbox{$\theta^{\alpha}|{\rm div}u|^{2}$}\hskip-20.9964pt\hskip-451.29065pt\raisebox{19.14015pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-3.34055pt}{\resizebox{53617400}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-853.45891pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-853.45891pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14236pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.98pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.7pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14285pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.7pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-451.29065pt\hskip 451.29065pt}\right|+|\xi|^{2}|\hbox{\hskip 451.29065pt\hskip-12.21164pt\hbox{$\theta^{\beta}\nabla\theta$}\hskip-12.21164pt\hskip-451.29065pt\raisebox{19.49014pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-3.34055pt}{\resizebox{53617400}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-853.45891pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-853.45891pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14236pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.98pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.7pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14285pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.7pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-451.29065pt\hskip 451.29065pt}|^{2}\Big)\,{\mathrm{d}}\xi

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\int_{S(t)}|\xi|^{2}|\hat{u}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}\xi,

(4.40)

which implies

\int_{S(t)}|\widehat{\dot{\theta}}(t)|^{2}\,{\mathrm{d}}\xi\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-1-r_{1}}.

Recalling the definition of
X(t)
in (
4.1
), from Young’s inequality and the fact that
\bar{\theta}
is large enough, we have

\displaystyle\overline{C}_{1}^{-1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\rho-\bar{\rho},u,\theta-\bar{\theta})\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\|(\dot{u},\dot{\theta})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq X(t)

\displaystyle\leq\overline{C}_{1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\rho-\bar{\rho},u,\theta-\bar{\theta})\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{2}+\|(\dot{u},\dot{\theta})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

(4.41)

for some constant
\overline{C}_{1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})>0
. Recalling that
S(t)
is the ball in
\mathbb{R}^{3}
centered at the origin with radius
\displaystyle r(t)=\left(\frac{C_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{1+t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}
, a decomposition of the frequency domain into two time-dependent subdomains by
S(t)^{c}
and
S(t)
yields that

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}X(t)+\frac{C_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})c_{1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{1+t}\left(\|u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\dot{u}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\dot{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{1+t}\int_{S(t)}\left(|\widehat{\rho-\bar{\rho}}(t)|^{2}+|\widehat{u}(t)|^{2}+|\widehat{\theta-\bar{\theta}}(t)|^{2}+|\widehat{\dot{u}}(t)|^{2}+|\widehat{\dot{\theta}}(t)|^{2}\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\xi.

(4.42)

Taking
C_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})=4\overline{C}_{1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})c_{1}^{-1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})
and adding (
4.11
) to (
4.1
), from (
4.1
) we get

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}X(t)+\frac{2}{1+t}X(t)\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{1+t}\int_{S(t)}\left(|\widehat{\rho-\bar{\rho}}|^{2}+|\widehat{u}|^{2}+|\widehat{\theta-\bar{\theta}}|^{2}+|\widehat{\dot{u}}|^{2}+|\widehat{\dot{\theta}}|^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}\xi

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-1-r_{1}},\qquad\text{for}\quad t\geq C_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta}).

(4.43)

Multiplying by the integrating factor
(1+t)^{2}
gives

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left[(1+t)^{2}X(t)\right]\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{1-r_{1}},\qquad\text{for}\quad t\geq C_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta}),

(4.44)

which leads to

X(t)\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-r_{1}},

(4.45)

and

\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{H^{1}}+\|u\|_{H^{1}}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+\|(\dot{u},\dot{\theta})\|_{L^{2}}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-r_{1}/2}.

(4.46)

Step 2
: We want to improve the decay estimate if
\beta(p_{0})>\frac{1}{4}
. By definition,
r_{1}=\frac{1}{2}
. Thanks to (
4.3
) and (
4.46
), we improve the estimate for the low frequency part as follows

\displaystyle\int_{S(t)}\left(|\widehat{\rho-\bar{\rho}}|^{2}+|\widehat{\rho u}|^{2}+|\hbox{\hskip 451.29065pt\hskip-14.09895pt\hbox{$\rho(\theta-\bar{\theta})$}\hskip-14.09895pt\hskip-451.29065pt\raisebox{18.50012pt}{\hbox{$\hbox{\raisebox{-3.34055pt}{\resizebox{53617400}{141084}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\mathchoice{\raisebox{-853.45891pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-853.45891pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{10.00012pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14236pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.4pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.98pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.7pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}{\raisebox{-157.14285pt}{\resizebox{468892385}{36044800}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\displaystyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{1.0pt}{$\textstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.7pt}{$\scriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\resizebox{0}{0}{\hbox{\raisebox{0.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern-0.6pt\bigwedge\kern-0.6pt$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}}}}$}}\hskip-451.29065pt\hskip 451.29065pt}|^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}\xi

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left((1+t)^{-2\beta({p_{0}})}+(1+t)^{-1}+(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\ln(1+t)\right).

(4.47)

Now following the similar argument used in the previous step, we conclude that

\displaystyle\int_{S(t)}\left(|\widehat{\rho-\bar{\rho}}|^{2}+|\widehat{u}|^{2}+|\widehat{\theta-\bar{\theta}}|^{2}+|\widehat{\dot{u}}|^{2}+|\widehat{\dot{\theta}}|^{2}\right)\,{\mathrm{d}}\xi

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left((1+t)^{-2\beta({p_{0}})}+(1+t)^{-1}+(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\ln(1+t)\right),

(4.48)

which implies that

\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}X(t)+\frac{2}{1+t}X(t)\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-1}\left((1+t)^{-2\beta({p_{0}})}+(1+t)^{-1}+(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\ln(1+t)\right).

(4.49)

We obtain that

X(t)\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\max\left\{(1+t)^{-2\beta({p_{0}})},(1+t)^{-1}\right\}=C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-r_{2}},

(4.50)

with
r_{2}=\min\{2\beta(p_{0}),1\}
, and

\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{H^{1}}+\|u\|_{H^{1}}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+\|(\dot{u},\dot{\theta})\|_{L^{2}}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-r_{2}/2}.

Step 3
: Finally we deal with the case that
\beta(p_{0})>\frac{1}{2}
. By (
4.50
), we have

\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{H^{1}}+\|u\|_{H^{1}}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}+\|(\dot{u},\dot{\theta})\|_{L^{2}}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{1}{2}}.

(4.51)

We may repeat the same process in the above to get that

\int_{S(t)}\left(|\widehat{\rho-\bar{\rho}}|^{2}+|\widehat{u}|^{2}+|\widehat{\theta-\bar{\theta}}|^{2}+|\widehat{\dot{u}}|^{2}+|\widehat{\dot{\theta}}|^{2}\right){\mathrm{d}}\xi\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left((1+t)^{-2\beta({p_{0}})}+(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right),

(4.52)

which implies that

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}X(t)+\frac{2}{1+t}X(t)\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-1}(1+t)^{-2\beta({p_{0}})}.

(4.53)

As a result, we can derive (
4.35
) easily from the above inequality.
∎

4.2
Decay rate of
\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{L^{2}}

First, we rewrite (
1.15
) as

\begin{cases}\rho_{t}+\bar{\rho}{\rm div}u=-\mathcal{M}_{1},\\
u_{t}+R\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}\nabla\rho+R\nabla\theta-\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\Delta u-(\mu+\lambda)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\nabla{\rm div}u=-\mathcal{M}_{2},\\
\theta_{t}+R\bar{\theta}{\rm div}u-\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\Delta\theta=-\mathcal{M}_{3},\end{cases}

(4.54)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{1}=~

\displaystyle u\cdot\nabla\rho+(\rho-\bar{\rho}){\rm div}u,

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{2}=~

\displaystyle(u\cdot\nabla)u+R\left(\rho^{-1}\theta-\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}\right)\nabla\rho-\mu\left(2\rho^{-1}{\rm div}(\theta^{\alpha}\mathfrak{D}(u))-\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}(\Delta u+\nabla{\rm div}u)\right)

\displaystyle-\lambda\left(\rho^{-1}\nabla(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u)-\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\nabla{\rm div}u\right),

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{3}=~

\displaystyle u\cdot\nabla\theta+R(\theta-\bar{\theta}){\rm div}u+2\mu\rho^{-1}\theta^{\alpha}|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}+\lambda\rho^{-1}\theta^{\alpha}({\rm div}u)^{2}-\kappa\left(\rho^{-1}{\rm div}(\theta^{\beta}\nabla\theta)-\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\Delta\theta\right).

(4.55)

Then we establish the estimate for the first order spatial derivative of the solution.

Lemma 4.5
.

The following estimate holds for the global solution
(\rho,u,\theta)
of (
4.54
) when
t\geq 1
.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right)\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.56)

Proof.

Taking the
L^{2}
-inner product of (
4.54
)
1
with
-\Delta\rho
, we have

\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\bar{\rho}\int\nabla^{2}\rho:\nabla u{\mathrm{d}}x=\int\mathcal{M}_{1}\Delta\rho{\mathrm{d}}x\leq\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathcal{M}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}.

(4.57)

Direct computation gives

\|\mathcal{M}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|u\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{6}}+\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{3}}\|{\rm div}u\|_{L^{6}}\leq C\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u)\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}\right).

(4.58)

Substituting (
4.58
) into (
4.57
), we get

\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\bar{\rho}\int\nabla^{2}\rho:\nabla u{\mathrm{d}}x\leq C\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u)\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.59)

Taking the
L^{2}
-inner product of (
4.54
)
2
with
-\Delta u
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla{\rm div}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+R\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}\rho:\nabla u{\mathrm{d}}x+R\int\nabla^{2}\theta:\nabla u{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathcal{M}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}.

(4.60)

Direct calculation gives

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{M}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\leq~

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{6}}+C\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla\theta)\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}.

(4.61)

Substituting (
4.2
) into (
4.2
) and using (
3.1.2
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla{\rm div}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+R\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}\rho:\nabla u{\mathrm{d}}x+R\int\nabla^{2}\theta:\nabla u{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2}{3}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.62)

Taking the
L^{2}
-inner product of (
4.54
)
3
with
-\Delta\theta
, we have

\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-R\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}\theta:\nabla u{\mathrm{d}}x\leq\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\mathcal{M}_{3}\|_{L^{2}}.

(4.63)

Direct calculation gives

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{M}_{3}\|_{L^{2}}\leq~

\displaystyle\|u\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{6}}+R\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{3}}\|{\rm div}u\|_{L^{6}}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+C\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right)\left(\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.64)

Substituting (
4.2
) into (
4.63
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-R\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}\theta:\nabla u{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right)\left(\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.65)

Making the linear combination (
4.59
)
\times R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}+
(
4.2
)
+
(
4.2
)
\times\bar{\theta}^{-1}
, we complete the proof.
∎

Now we establish the energy estimate for the second order spatial derivative of the solution.

Lemma 4.6
.

The following estimate holds for the global solution
(\rho,u,\theta)
of (
4.54
) when
t\geq 1
.

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right)\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\right).

(4.66)

Proof.

Applying
\nabla^{2}
operator to (
4.54
)
1
, and taking the
L^{2}
-inner product with
\nabla^{2}\rho
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\rho}\int\nabla^{2}\rho:\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\int\nabla^{2}\rho:\nabla^{2}(u\cdot\nabla\rho){\mathrm{d}}x-\int\nabla^{2}\rho:\nabla^{2}((\rho-\bar{\rho}){\rm div}u){\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int u\cdot\nabla(|\nabla^{2}\rho|^{2}){\mathrm{d}}x+C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\left(\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}\right)\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2}{3}}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\right)\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

(4.67)

where we have used (
3.131
). Applying
\nabla^{2}
operator to (
4.54
)
2
, and taking the
L^{2}
-inner product with
\nabla^{2}u
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}\rho:\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x-R\int\nabla^{2}\theta:\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}.

(4.68)

Direct calculation gives

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\leq~

\displaystyle\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{6}}+C\bar{\rho}^{-1}\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{6}}+C\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{6}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}+\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\times

\displaystyle\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}\right)+C(\bar{\theta})\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right)\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}\right).

(4.69)

Inserting (
4.2
) into (
4.2
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}\rho:\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x-R\int\nabla^{2}\theta:\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right)\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.70)

Applying
\nabla^{2}
operator to (
4.54
)
3
, and taking the
L^{2}
-inner product with
\nabla^{2}\theta
, we get

\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+R\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}\theta:\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x\leq\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{3}\|_{L^{2}}.

(4.71)

Direct calculation gives

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{3}\|_{L^{2}}\leq~

\displaystyle\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{6}}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+C\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\alpha-1}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{6}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{6}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{\infty}}+C\bar{\theta}^{\beta-2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right)\left(\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}\right).

(4.72)

Substituting (
4.2
) into (
4.71
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+R\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}\theta:\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{4}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right)\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.73)

Making the linear combination (
4.2
)
\times R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}+
(
4.2
)
+
(
4.2
)
\times\bar{\theta}^{-1}
, we complete the proof.
∎

In order to close the estimates, we need to establish the following dissipation estimate for

\nabla^{2}\rho
.

Lemma 4.7
.

The following estimate holds for the global solution
(\rho,u,\theta)
of (
4.54
) when
t\geq 1
.

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int\nabla u:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{R\bar{\theta}}{2\bar{\rho}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\overline{C}_{2}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right)\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.74)

Proof.

Applying
\nabla
operator to (
4.54
)
2
, and taking the
L^{2}
-inner product with
\nabla^{2}\rho
, we get

\displaystyle\int\nabla u_{t}:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x+R\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+R\int\nabla^{2}\theta:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x-\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int\nabla\Delta u:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-(\mu+\lambda)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x=-\int\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2}:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x.

(4.75)

Using (
4.54
)
1
, we have

\displaystyle\int\nabla u_{t}:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x=~

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int\nabla u:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x+\int\nabla{\rm div}u\cdot\nabla\rho_{t}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~
