\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int\nabla u:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x-\bar{\rho}\|\nabla{\rm div}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\int\nabla{\rm div}u\cdot\nabla\mathcal{M}_{1}{\mathrm{d}}x.

(4.76)

Hence we get

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int\nabla u:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x+R\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-R\int\nabla^{2}\theta:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int\nabla\Delta u:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x+(\mu+\lambda)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\bar{\rho}\|\nabla{\rm div}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int\nabla{\rm div}u\cdot\nabla\mathcal{M}_{1}{\mathrm{d}}x-\int\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2}:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{R\bar{\theta}}{2\bar{\rho}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\mathcal{M}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.77)

Substituting (
4.58
) and (
4.2
) into (
4.2
), we complete the proof.
∎

Combining all the estimates obtained in Lemmas
4.2
–
4.2
, we derive the following energy estimate.

Lemma 4.8
.

For the global solution
(\rho,u,\theta)
, there exist energy functional
\mathcal{E}(t)\sim\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}
, time
T_{1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})>0
and a positive constant
K_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})
, such that for any
t\geq T_{1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})
, it holds

\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}(t)+K_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq 0.

(4.78)

Proof.

For fixed
\delta(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})>0
, adding (
4.5
), (
4.6
) and
\delta(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\times
(
4.7
) together, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+2\delta(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\int\nabla u:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\frac{R\bar{\theta}}{2\bar{\rho}}\delta(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\right)\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\overline{C}_{2}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\delta(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.79)

Taking
\delta(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})
sufficiently small, such that

\overline{C}_{2}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\delta(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\leq\frac{1}{2}\min\left\{\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha},~\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\right\},

and

2\delta(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left|\int\nabla u:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x\right|\leq\frac{1}{2}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\right),

we obtain the following inequality

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}(t)+2K_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

(4.80)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\right)\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\right),

where

\mathcal{E}(t):=\frac{1}{2}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\right)+\delta(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\int\nabla u:\nabla^{2}\rho{\mathrm{d}}x\sim\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}.

(4.81)

Noticing (
4.1
), we can see that there exists a constant
T_{1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})>0
, such that for any
t\geq T_{1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})
,

C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\right)\leq K_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta}).

(4.82)

Combining (
4.80
) and (
4.82
), we complete the proof of Lemma
4.2
.
∎

At last, we will use the same manner as
[
15
]
to prove (
1.22
). More precisely, we shall prove the following decay estimate.

Lemma 4.9
.

Suppose the condition of Theorem
1.1
and Theorem
1.2
hold. Let
(\rho,u,\theta)
be the global strong solution obtained in Theorem
1.1
. Then we have

\|\rho_{t}\|_{L^{2}}+\|u_{t}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\theta_{t}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}.

(4.83)

Proof.

First, we rewrite (
4.78
) as follows,

\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}(t)+\frac{K_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{2}\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\frac{K_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{2}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq 0.

(4.84)

Next, we denote the time-dependent domain

\tilde{S}(t):=\left\{\xi\in{\mathbb{R}}^{3}:~|\xi|^{2}\leq\frac{c_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{1+t}\right\},

with a constant
c_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})
to be determined later, and split the whole space
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}
into two time-dependent regions
\tilde{S}(t)
and
{\mathbb{R}}^{3}\setminus\tilde{S}(t)
. Now we can estimate the second term in (
4.84
) as follows.

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\int_{\tilde{S}(t)}\left[(|\xi|^{6}+|\xi|^{4})\left(|\widehat{u}|^{2}+|\widehat{\theta-\bar{\theta}}|^{2}\right)+|\xi|^{4}|\widehat{\rho-\bar{\rho}}|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\xi

\displaystyle+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}\setminus\tilde{S}(t)}\left[(|\xi|^{6}+|\xi|^{4})\left(|\widehat{u}|^{2}+|\widehat{\theta-\bar{\theta}}|^{2}\right)+|\xi|^{4}|\widehat{\rho-\bar{\rho}}|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\xi

\displaystyle\geq~

\displaystyle\frac{c_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{1+t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}\setminus\tilde{S}(t)}\left[(|\xi|^{4}+|\xi|^{2})\left(|\widehat{u}|^{2}+|\widehat{\theta-\bar{\theta}}|^{2}\right)+|\xi|^{2}|\widehat{\rho-\bar{\rho}}|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\xi

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\frac{c_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{1+t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{3}}\left[(|\xi|^{4}+|\xi|^{2})\left(|\widehat{u}|^{2}+|\widehat{\theta-\bar{\theta}}|^{2}\right)+|\xi|^{2}|\widehat{\rho-\bar{\rho}}|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\xi

\displaystyle-\frac{c_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{1+t}\int_{\tilde{S}(t)}\left[(|\xi|^{4}+|\xi|^{2})\left(|\widehat{u}|^{2}+|\widehat{\theta-\bar{\theta}}|^{2}\right)+|\xi|^{2}|\widehat{\rho-\bar{\rho}}|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\xi

\displaystyle\geq~

\displaystyle\frac{c_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{1+t}\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{c_{*}^{2}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{(1+t)^{2}}\int_{\tilde{S}(t)}\left[(|\xi|^{2}+1)\left(|\widehat{u}|^{2}+|\widehat{\theta-\bar{\theta}}|^{2}\right)+|\widehat{\rho-\bar{\rho}}|^{2}\right]{\mathrm{d}}\xi

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\frac{c_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{1+t}\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)-\frac{c_{*}^{2}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{(1+t)^{2}}\left(\|u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

(4.85)

Substituting (
4.2
) into (
4.84
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}(t)+\frac{K_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{2}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{K_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})c_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{2(1+t)}\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{K_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})c_{*}^{2}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{2(1+t)^{2}}\left(\|u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.86)

Denote
T_{2}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})=c_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})-1
. Then for any
t\geq\max\{1,T_{1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta}),T_{2}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\}
, from (
4.1
) we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}(t)+\frac{K_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})c_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{2(1+t)}\mathcal{E}(t)\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}{(1+t)^{2}}\left(\|u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2}.

(4.87)

Taking
c_{*}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})=\frac{6}{K_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})}
, we get

\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}(t)+\frac{3\mathcal{E}(t)}{1+t}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2},

(4.88)

from which we can easily obtain

\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}.

(4.89)

From (
4.54
), we have

\|\rho_{t}\|_{L^{2}}+\|u_{t}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\theta_{t}\|_{L^{2}}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}+\|\mathcal{M}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\mathcal{M}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\mathcal{M}_{3}\|_{L^{2}}\right).

(4.90)

Therefore, (
4.83
) follows from (
4.90
), (
4.89
), (
4.58
), (
4.2
) and (
4.2
).
∎

4.3
Decay rate of
\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{2}u,\nabla^{2}\theta)\|_{L^{2}}

To estimate the second-order derivatives of the solution, we need the following
L^{2}
-time decay rate of the linearized system of (
4.54
), which can be found in
[
30
]
.

Proposition 4.10
.

Supposed that
U(t)=(\rho,u,\theta)^{\top}
is the solution of the linearized system of (
4.54
)

\begin{cases}\rho_{t}+\bar{\rho}{\rm div}u=0,\\
u_{t}+R\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}\nabla\rho+R\nabla\theta-\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\Delta u-(\mu+\lambda)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\nabla{\rm div}u=0,\\
\theta_{t}+R\bar{\theta}{\rm div}u-\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\Delta\theta=0,\end{cases}

with initial data
(\rho_{0},u_{0},\theta_{0})
satisfying

(\rho_{0}-\bar{\rho},u_{0},\theta_{0}-\bar{\theta})\in H^{2}({\mathbb{R}}^{3})\cap L^{p_{0}}({\mathbb{R}}^{3}),

then for any
t\geq 0
, it holds that

\left\|\nabla^{k}U(t)\right\|_{L^{2}}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{k}{2}}\|U(0)\|_{H^{2}\cap L^{p_{0}}},\quad\text{for}\quad k=0,1,2.

(4.91)

Now we choose a radial function
\phi(\xi)\in C_{c}^{\infty}({\mathbb{R}}^{3})
, such that

\phi(\xi)\equiv 0,\quad\text{for}\quad|\xi|\geq 2,\quad\text{and}\quad\phi(\xi)\equiv 1,\quad\text{for}\quad|\xi|\leq 1.

We define the low-frequency part and the high-frequency part of a given function
f(x)
as follows,

f_{L}:=\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left(\phi(\xi)\widehat{f}(\xi)\right),\quad\text{and}\quad f_{H}:=\mathcal{F}^{-1}\left((1-\phi(\xi))\widehat{f}(\xi)\right)=f-f_{L}.

(4.92)

Next, we shall establish the
L^{2}
-decay rate of
(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{2}u,\nabla^{2}\theta)
when
(\rho_{0}-\bar{\rho},u_{0},\theta_{0}-\bar{\theta})\in L^{p_{0}}
for some
p_{0}\in[1,2]
. We will deal with the case
1\leq p_{0}<2
and
p_{0}=2
separately.

4.3.1
Decay rate for the case
1\leq p_{0}<2

In this subsection, we will focus on the case
1\leq p_{0}<2
. The following lemma is concerned with the optimal decay rate on
\big\|\big(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{2}u,\nabla^{2}\theta\big)_{L}\big\|_{L^{2}}
.

Lemma 4.11
.

For the global solution
(\rho,u,\theta)
obtained in Theorem
1.1
, when
(\rho_{0}-\bar{\rho},u_{0},\theta_{0}-\bar{\theta})\in L^{p_{0}}
for
p_{0}\in[1,2)
, it holds that

\big\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{2}u,\nabla^{2}\theta)_{L}\big\|_{L^{2}}\leq~C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}.

(4.93)

Proof.

By Lemma
4.3
and Duhamel’s principle, we have

\displaystyle\big\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{2}u,\nabla^{2}\theta)_{L}\big\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\int_{0}^{t}(1+t-\tau)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}\big\|(\mathcal{M}_{1},\mathcal{M}_{2},\mathcal{M}_{3})_{L}(\tau)\big\|_{H^{2}\cap L^{p_{0}}}{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\int_{0}^{t}(1+t-\tau)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}\big\|(\mathcal{M}_{1},\mathcal{M}_{2},\mathcal{M}_{3})(\tau)\big\|_{L^{p_{0}}\cap L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\int_{0}^{t}(1+t-\tau)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}\big\|(\mathcal{M}_{1},\mathcal{M}_{2},\mathcal{M}_{3})(\tau)\big\|_{L^{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\int_{0}^{t}(1+t-\tau)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}\big\|(\mathcal{M}_{1},\mathcal{M}_{2},\mathcal{M}_{3})(\tau)\big\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1}\big\|(\mathcal{M}_{1},\mathcal{M}_{2},\mathcal{M}_{3})(\tau)\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2-\frac{2}{p_{0}}}{\mathrm{d}}\tau.

(4.94)

By the definition of
\mathcal{M}_{i}~(i=1,2,3)
, we have

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathcal{M}_{1},\mathcal{M}_{2},\mathcal{M}_{3})(t)\big\|_{L^{1}}\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\rho-\bar{\rho},u,\theta-\bar{\theta})\|_{L^{2}}\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{L^{2}}+\|(\nabla\theta,\nabla u)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}\right)\|(\nabla^{2}u,\nabla^{2}\theta)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}},

(4.95)

and

\displaystyle\big\|(\mathcal{M}_{1},\mathcal{M}_{2},\mathcal{M}_{3})(t)\big\|_{L^{2}}\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\rho-\bar{\rho},u,\theta-\bar{\theta})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{L^{2}}+\|(\nabla\theta,\nabla u)\|_{L^{4}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\|(\nabla^{2}u,\nabla^{2}\theta)\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}.

(4.96)

Substituting (
4.3.1
)–(
4.3.1
) into (
4.3.1
) and noticing that

\displaystyle\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)\left[-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}\right]+\left(2-\frac{2}{p_{0}}\right)\left[-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1\right]

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)+\frac{1}{p_{0}}-\frac{3}{2}

\displaystyle<~

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1,

we get

\displaystyle\big\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{2}u,\nabla^{2}\theta)_{L}\big\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\int_{0}^{t}(1+t-\tau)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}(1+\tau)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\int_{0}^{t}(1+t-\tau)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}(1+\tau)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)+\frac{1}{p_{0}}-\frac{3}{2}}{\mathrm{d}}\tau

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}.

Therefore, we get the desired estimate (
4.93
).
∎

Next, we will establish the decay rate on the high-frequency of the second-order spatial derivatives of solution. The proof mainly involves the following three steps, where the first step is the following high-frequency energy estimate.

Lemma 4.12
.

For the global solution
(\rho,u,\theta)
, it holds

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|(\nabla^{2}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{3}u,\nabla^{3}\theta)\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)}\left\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{3}u,\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.97)

Proof.

Applying
\nabla^{2}
operator to (
4.54
), then taking the high-frequency part, then taking the
L^{2}
-inner product with
(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{2}u,\bar{\theta}^{-1}\nabla^{2}\theta)_{H}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|(\nabla^{2}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|(\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int(\nabla^{2}\mathcal{M}_{1})_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+\int(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2})_{H}:(\nabla\Delta u)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\int(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{3})_{H}\cdot(\nabla\Delta\theta)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}(u\cdot\nabla\rho)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}((\rho-\bar{\rho}){\rm div}u)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\int\nabla(u\cdot\nabla u)_{H}:(\nabla\Delta u)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+R\int\nabla\left((\rho^{-1}\theta-\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta})\nabla\rho\right)_{H}:(\nabla\Delta u)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-\mu\int\nabla\left(2\rho^{-1}{\rm div}(\theta^{\alpha}\mathfrak{D}(u))-\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}(\Delta u+\nabla{\rm div}u)\right)_{H}:(\nabla\Delta u)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-\lambda\int\nabla\left(\rho^{-1}\nabla(\theta^{\alpha}{\rm div}u)-\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\nabla{\rm div}u\right)_{H}:(\nabla\Delta u)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\int\nabla(u\cdot\nabla\theta)_{H}\cdot(\nabla\Delta\theta)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+R\bar{\theta}^{-1}\int\nabla\left((\theta-\bar{\theta}){\rm div}u\right)_{H}\cdot(\nabla\Delta\theta)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+2\mu\bar{\theta}^{-1}\int\nabla\left(\rho^{-1}\theta^{\alpha}|\mathfrak{D}(u)|^{2}\right)_{H}\cdot(\nabla\Delta\theta)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\lambda\bar{\theta}^{-1}\int\nabla\left(\rho^{-1}\theta^{\alpha}({\rm div}u)^{2}\right)_{H}\cdot(\nabla\Delta\theta)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x-\kappa\bar{\theta}^{-1}\int\nabla\left(\rho^{-1}{\rm div}(\theta^{\beta}\nabla\theta)-\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\Delta\theta\right)_{H}\cdot(\nabla\Delta\theta)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=:~

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{i=1}^{11}H_{i},

(4.98)

where the terms
H_{i}
can be estimated as follows.

\displaystyle H_{1}=~

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}(u\cdot\nabla\rho):(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}(u\cdot\nabla\rho)_{L}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}(u\cdot(\nabla\rho)_{L}):(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}(u\cdot(\nabla\rho)_{H}):(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}(u\cdot\nabla\rho)_{L}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}(u\cdot(\nabla\rho)_{L}):(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}u\cdot(\nabla\rho)_{H}\cdot(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla u\cdot(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int(u\cdot\nabla)(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}(u\cdot\nabla\rho)_{L}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}(u\cdot(\nabla\rho)_{L}):(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}u\cdot(\nabla\rho)_{H}\cdot(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla u\cdot(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}}{2}\int{\rm div}u|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}(u\cdot\nabla\rho)_{L}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}\right)\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla^{2}(u\cdot\nabla\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}\right)\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla(u\cdot\nabla\rho)\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\left(\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

(4.99)

\displaystyle H_{2}=~

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}((\rho-\bar{\rho}){\rm div}u)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla^{2}((\rho-\bar{\rho}){\rm div}u)\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{6}}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}\right)\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}\right)\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

(4.100)

\displaystyle H_{3}=~

\displaystyle\int\nabla(u\cdot\nabla u)_{H}:(\nabla\Delta u)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\|\nabla(u\cdot\nabla u)\|_{L^{2}}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\right)\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(4.101)

\displaystyle H_{4}=~

\displaystyle R\int\nabla\left((\rho^{-1}\theta-\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta})\nabla\rho\right)_{H}:(\nabla\Delta u)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\nabla((\rho^{-1}\theta-\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta})\nabla\rho)\|_{L^{2}}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\left(\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}+\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}\right)\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{6}}\right)\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}\right)\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(4.102)

\displaystyle H_{5}+H_{6}\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\left(\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}+\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}\right)\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{6}}\right)\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{6}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\right)\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{3}u,\nabla^{3}\theta)\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)}\left\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(4.103)

\displaystyle H_{7}+H_{8}\leq~

\displaystyle C\left(\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{4}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{4}}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}\right)\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(4.104)

\displaystyle H_{9}+H_{10}\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{6}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{6}}\right)\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}\left(\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

(4.105)

\displaystyle H_{11}\leq~

\displaystyle C\left\|\nabla\left(\rho^{-1}{\rm div}(\theta^{\beta}\nabla\theta)-\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta}\Delta\theta\right)\right\|_{L^{2}}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\left(\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}\right)\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{6}}\right)\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{6}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{6}}\right)\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.106)

Substituting (
4.3.1
)–(
4.3.1
) into (
4.3.1
), we complete the proof of (
4.12
).
∎

The next lemma is devoted to establishing the dissipation estimate of
(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}
.

Lemma 4.13
.

For the global solution
(\rho,u,\theta)
, it holds

\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int(\nabla u)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{R\bar{\theta}}{2\bar{\rho}}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left\|(\nabla^{3}u,\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.107)

Proof.

Applying
\nabla
operator to (
4.54
)
2
, then taking the high-frequency part, then taking the
L^{2}
-inner product with
(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int(\nabla u)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+R\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-R\int(\nabla^{2}\theta)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int(\nabla\Delta u)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+(\mu+\lambda)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int(\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\bar{\rho}\|(\nabla{\rm div}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\int(\mathcal{M}_{1})_{H}(\Delta{\rm div}u)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x-\int(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2})_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{R\bar{\theta}}{2\bar{\rho}}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla^{2}u)_{H}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\mathcal{M}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{R\bar{\theta}}{2\bar{\rho}}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\mathcal{M}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.108)

Similar to (
4.58
) and (
4.2
), we have

\|\mathcal{M}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}+\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\leq C\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1},

(4.109)

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}+\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}+\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\times

\displaystyle\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}\right)+C(\bar{\theta})\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}\right)\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{H^{1}}+\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-1}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}},

(4.110)

where we have used (
4.89
) and Lemma
4.3.1
. At last, inserting (
4.109
) and (
4.3.1
) into (
4.3.1
), we complete the proof of (
4.107
).
∎

At last, we use the same manner as
[
40
]
to prove (
1.23
) when
1\leq p_{0}<2
.

Proof.

Similar to Lemma
4.2
, by making suitable linear combination of (
4.12
) and (
4.107
), we conclude that there exist energy functional
\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)\sim\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{2}u,\nabla^{2}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
, a large time
T_{3}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})>0
and a positive constant
\tilde{c}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})
, such that for any
t\geq T_{3}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})
, it holds

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)+2\tilde{c}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{3}u,\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{3}u,\nabla^{3}\theta)\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{3}u,\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{3}u,\nabla^{3}\theta)_{L}\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2}+\tilde{c}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{3}u,\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{4}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{2}u,\nabla^{2}\theta)_{L}\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2}+\tilde{c}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{3}u,\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\right\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(4.111)

where we have used Lemma
4.3.1
and the largeness of
T_{3}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})
.

Noticing that

\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)\sim\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{2}u,\nabla^{2}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{3}u,\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

we obtain

\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\mathcal{E}_{1}(t)\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})(1+t)^{-\frac{3}{2}\left(\frac{2}{p_{0}}-1\right)-2}.

(4.112)

Then the desired decay estimate follows from (
4.112
) and Lemma
4.3.1
immediately.
∎

4.3.2
Decay rate for the case
p_{0}=2

In this subsection, we will focus on the case
p_{0}=2
, which requires more accurate estimates on both the low-frequency and the high-frequency part. The following lemma is devoted to establishing energy estimates of the second-order derivatives of the solution.

Lemma 4.14
.

For the global solution
(\rho,u,\theta)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|(\nabla^{2}\theta)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\varepsilon_{0}^{-1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(4.113)

and

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|(\nabla^{2}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\varepsilon_{0}^{-1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(4.114)

with some positive constant
\varepsilon_{0}
depending on
\bar{\rho}
,
\bar{\theta}
to be determined later.

Proof.

Applying
\nabla^{2}
operator to (
4.54
), then taking the low-frequency part, then taking the
L^{2}
-inner product with
(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{2}u,\bar{\theta}^{-1}\nabla^{2}\theta)_{L}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|(\nabla^{2}\theta)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|(\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{1})_{L}\cdot(\nabla\Delta\rho)_{L}{\mathrm{d}}x+\int(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2})_{L}:(\nabla\Delta u)_{L}{\mathrm{d}}x+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\int(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{3})_{L}\cdot(\nabla\Delta\theta)_{L}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\mu\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}}{2\bar{\rho}}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\kappa\bar{\theta}^{\beta}}{2\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\varepsilon_{0}^{-1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{1})_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2})_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{3})_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.115)

Recalling the definition of
\mathcal{M}_{i}~(i=1,2,3)
in (
4.2
), by Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality and Sobolev embedding inequalities, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\cdot\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\rho-\bar{\rho})\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\Big(\|\nabla u\cdot(\nabla\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\cdot(\nabla\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|u(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\rho-\bar{\rho})\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\Big(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}^{2}\|(\nabla\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}^{2}\|(\nabla\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|u\|_{L^{3}}^{2}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{3}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\Big(\|\nabla u\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}\|(\nabla\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u)\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

(4.116)

and

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\Big(\|\nabla u\cdot\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\rho-\bar{\rho})\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|(\theta-\bar{\theta})\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\rho-\bar{\rho})\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\theta-\bar{\theta})\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla u\cdot\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\cdot\nabla u\cdot\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\rho-\bar{\rho})\nabla^{2}u\cdot\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\rho-\bar{\rho})\nabla u\cdot\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|(\theta-\bar{\theta})\nabla^{2}u\cdot\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\theta-\bar{\theta})\nabla u\cdot\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big):=\sum\limits_{i=1}^{16}\widetilde{I}_{i},

(4.117)

where the norms on the right-hand side can be estimated as follows.

\displaystyle\widetilde{I}_{1}+\widetilde{I}_{2}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla u\|_{L^{4}}^{4}+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{3}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(4.118)

\displaystyle\widetilde{I}_{3}\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla\rho)_{L}\cdot\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla\rho)_{H}\cdot\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}^{2}\|(\nabla\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{3}}^{2}\|(\nabla\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(\rho-\bar{\rho})_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4}{3}}+\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla\theta\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

(4.119)

\displaystyle\widetilde{I}_{4}+\widetilde{I}_{5}+\widetilde{I}_{6}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla\rho)_{H}\cdot\nabla\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla\rho)_{L}\cdot(\nabla\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}^{2}\|(\nabla\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+\|(\nabla\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{4}}^{4}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{3}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla\rho\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

(4.120)

\displaystyle\widetilde{I}_{7}+\widetilde{I}_{8}+\widetilde{I}_{9}+\widetilde{I}_{10}\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla\theta)\|_{L^{3}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+\|(\rho-\bar{\rho},\theta-\bar{\theta})\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(4.121)

\displaystyle\widetilde{I}_{11}+\widetilde{I}_{12}\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla\theta)\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{6}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\left(\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

(4.122)

\displaystyle\widetilde{I}_{13}+\widetilde{I}_{14}+\widetilde{I}_{15}+\widetilde{I}_{16}\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\rho-\bar{\rho},\theta-\bar{\theta})\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\left(\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\nabla\theta\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{6}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla\theta)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\left(\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.123)

Similar to (
4.3.2
)–(
4.3.2
), we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{3}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\Big(\|\nabla u\cdot\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\theta-\bar{\theta})\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla u\cdot\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla u\cdot\nabla\theta\cdot\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\cdot\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\rho-\bar{\rho})\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\theta-\bar{\theta})\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla\theta\cdot\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla\theta\cdot\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\theta\cdot\nabla\theta\cdot\nabla\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.124)

Inserting (
4.3.2
)–(
4.3.2
) into (
4.3.2
), we have proved (
4.14
).

Applying
\nabla^{2}
operator to (
4.54
), then taking the high-frequency part, then taking the
L^{2}
-inner product with
(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{2}u,\bar{\theta}^{-1}\nabla^{2}\theta)_{H}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|(\nabla^{2}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\mu+\lambda)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|(\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int(\nabla^{2}\mathcal{M}_{1})_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+\int(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2})_{H}:(\nabla\Delta u)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\int(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{3})_{H}\cdot(\nabla\Delta\theta)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{\mu\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}}{2\bar{\rho}}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\kappa\bar{\theta}^{\beta}}{2\bar{\rho}\bar{\theta}}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2})_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{3})_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int(\nabla^{2}\mathcal{M}_{1})_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x,

(4.125)

where we only need to estimate the last term on the right-hand side. Similar to (
4.3.1
)–(
4.3.1
), by Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality and Sobolev embedding inequalities, we have

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int(\nabla^{2}\mathcal{M}_{1})_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}(u\cdot(\nabla\rho)_{L}):(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}u\cdot(\nabla\rho)_{H}\cdot(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla u\cdot(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}}{2}\int{\rm div}u|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}|^{2}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}(u\cdot\nabla\rho)_{L}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x-R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\int\nabla^{2}((\rho-\bar{\rho}){\rm div}u)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\varepsilon_{0}^{-1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\Big(\|\nabla^{2}(u\cdot(\nabla\rho)_{L})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|(\nabla\rho)_{H}\cdot\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}(u\cdot\nabla\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}((\rho-\bar{\rho}){\rm div}u))\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\varepsilon_{0}^{-1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\Big(\|u(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\cdot(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla(u\cdot\nabla\rho)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}\rho\cdot\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\rho-\bar{\rho})\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\varepsilon_{0}^{-1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\Big(\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla\rho\|_{L^{3}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+\|\nabla u\|_{L^{3}}^{2}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{6}}^{2}+\|\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u\nabla^{2}\rho\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\cdot\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\rho-\bar{\rho}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\varepsilon_{0}^{-1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u)\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\varepsilon_{0}^{-1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\Big(\|\nabla\rho\cdot\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla u\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\varepsilon_{0}^{-1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u)\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\varepsilon_{0}^{-1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\Big(\|(\nabla\rho)_{L}\cdot\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla\rho)_{H}\cdot\nabla u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u\|_{L^{3}}^{2}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{6}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|u\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\varepsilon_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\varepsilon_{0}^{-1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u)\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.126)

Combining (
4.3.2
) and (
4.3.2
), we have proved (
4.14
).
∎

Next, we will establish dissipative estimates of
(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}
and
(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}
.

Lemma 4.15
.

For the global solution
(\rho,u,\theta)
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int(\nabla^{2}u)_{L}\cdot(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{R\bar{\theta}}{2\bar{\rho}}\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\overline{C}_{3}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

(4.127)

and

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int(\nabla u)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+\frac{R\bar{\theta}}{2\bar{\rho}}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\overline{C}_{3}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

(4.128)

for some positive constant
\overline{C}_{3}
depending on
\bar{\rho}
and
\bar{\theta}
.

Proof.

Applying
\nabla^{2}
operator to (
4.54
)
2
, then taking the low-frequency part, then taking the
L^{2}
-inner product with
(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int(\nabla^{2}u)_{L}\cdot(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}{\mathrm{d}}x+R\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-R\int(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{L}:(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}{\mathrm{d}}x+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int(\nabla^{2}\Delta u)_{L}\cdot(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}{\mathrm{d}}x+(\mu+\lambda)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int(\nabla^{3}{\rm div}u)_{L}\cdot(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\bar{\rho}\|(\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int(\nabla^{2}\mathcal{M}_{1})_{L}\cdot(\nabla\Delta u)_{L}{\mathrm{d}}x-\int(\nabla^{2}\mathcal{M}_{2})_{L}\cdot(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{R\bar{\theta}}{2\bar{\rho}}\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.129)

Substituting (
4.3.2
)–(
4.3.2
) into (
4.3.2
), we complete the proof of (
4.15
).

Applying
\nabla
operator to (
4.54
)
2
, then taking the high-frequency part, then taking the
L^{2}
-inner product with
(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\int(\nabla u)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+R\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle-R\int(\nabla^{2}\theta)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int(\nabla\Delta u)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x+(\mu+\lambda)\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\int(\nabla^{2}{\rm div}u)_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle+\bar{\rho}\|(\nabla{\rm div}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{1})_{H}\cdot(\Delta u)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x-\int(\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2})_{H}:(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{R\bar{\theta}}{2\bar{\rho}}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla^{2}u)_{H}\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle\frac{R\bar{\theta}}{2\bar{\rho}}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\mathcal{M}_{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.130)

Substituting (
4.3.2
)–(
4.3.2
) into (
4.3.2
), we complete the proof of (
4.15
).
∎

Now we are in a position to prove (
1.23
) when
p_{0}=2
.

Proof.

For fixed
\delta_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})>0
, adding
\delta_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\times
(
4.15
) to (
4.14
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|(\nabla^{2}\theta)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\int(\nabla^{2}u)_{L}\cdot(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}{\mathrm{d}}x\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha}\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\frac{R\bar{\theta}}{2\bar{\rho}}\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\varepsilon_{0}^{-1}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\varepsilon_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\overline{C}_{3}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.131)

Taking
\delta_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})
sufficiently small, such that

\delta_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\overline{C}_{3}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\leq\frac{1}{2}\min\left\{\mu\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\alpha},\kappa\bar{\rho}^{-1}\bar{\theta}^{\beta-1}\right\},

and

\delta_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left|\int(\nabla^{2}u)_{L}\cdot(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}{\mathrm{d}}x\right|\leq\frac{1}{4}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

then taking
4\bar{\rho}\varepsilon_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})=\delta_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})R\bar{\theta}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|(\nabla^{2}\theta)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\int(\nabla^{2}u)_{L}\cdot(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}{\mathrm{d}}x\right)

\displaystyle+2M_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right),

(4.132)

for some positive constant
M_{0}
depending on
\bar{\rho}
and
\bar{\theta}
. On the other hand, adding
\delta_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\times
(
4.15
) to (
4.14
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\left(R\bar{\rho}^{-2}\bar{\theta}\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\bar{\theta}^{-1}\|(\nabla^{2}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\delta_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\int(\nabla u)_{H}\cdot(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}{\mathrm{d}}x\right)

\displaystyle+2M_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla^{3}u)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\theta)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq~

\displaystyle C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla\rho,\nabla u,\nabla\theta)\|_{H^{1}}\left(\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

(4.133)

Adding (
4.3.2
) to (
4.3.2
) and noticing (
4.83
), one finds that there exist energy functional
\mathcal{E}_{2}(t)
equivalent to
\|(\nabla^{2}\rho,\nabla^{2}u,\nabla^{2}\theta)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
and a large time
T_{4}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})>0
, such that for any
t\geq T_{4}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})
, it holds

\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}_{2}(t)+M_{0}(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\left(\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)\leq 0.

(4.134)

From Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequality, we have

\displaystyle\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq~

\displaystyle C\|(\rho-\bar{\rho})_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4}{3}}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4}{3}},

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq~

\displaystyle C\|u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4}{3}}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4}{3}},

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq~

\displaystyle C\|\theta-\bar{\theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4}{3}}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{4}{3}},

(4.135)

hence we get

\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{3}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{3}\rho)_{L}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},~\|\nabla^{2}u\|_{L^{2}}^{3}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla^{3}u\|_{L^{2}}^{2},~\|\nabla^{2}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{3}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|\nabla^{3}\theta\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(4.136)

Inserting (
4.136
) into (
4.3.2
) and noting that
\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{3}\leq C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\|(\nabla^{2}\rho)_{H}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
, we obtain

\frac{{\mathrm{d}}}{{\mathrm{d}}t}\mathcal{E}_{2}(t)+C(\bar{\rho},\bar{\theta})\mathcal{E}_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}(t)\leq 0,

based on which we can derive the desired decay estimate (
1.23
) when
p_{0}=2
.
∎
